Abstract: Implicit multimodal in-context learning compresses demonstrations into internal interventions, ranging from static task vectors to query-conditioned transformations and attention routing. Despite their common goal, these methods differ substantially in how the intervention depends on the query and where it modifies the model, leaving unclear which additional complexity is necessary for a given task. We propose the Selection--Realization Hypothesis. It views demonstrations as inducing a compact family of internal changes from which the query selects, while the model's computation constrains how the selected change can be implemented. We evaluate this account using controlled multimodal tasks in which query dependence varies without changing the underlying task primitives or prompt format. By contrasting correct demonstrations with matched counterfactuals, we measure the structure of explicit M-ICL and test whether it predicts intervention behavior. We find that the success of a static task vector is closely tied to how much of the demonstration-induced change is shared across queries. Additional intervention complexity becomes useful when explicit M-ICL contains query-specific or distributed structure that a local additive shift cannot recover. These relationships extend to natural VQA benchmarks and support cost-aware method selection without access to test performance. Our results provide a unified empirical theory of when demonstrations can be compressed into a task vector and when a more expressive intervention is warranted.

</details>


### [75] [Context-Matched Distillation: Teacher Causality for Autoregressive Video Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13391)
*Hmrishav Bandyopadhyay,Xuanchi Ren,Zijian Huang,Jay Zhangjie Wu,Tianshi Cao,Ruilong Li,Bryan Chu,Sanja Fidler,Yi-Zhe Song,Zian Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出上下文匹配蒸馏（CMD），一种因果视频分布匹配蒸馏框架，通过用因果教师替代双向教师进行监督，使教师评分与学生生成时可用信息一致，解决了现有DMD流程中因果few-step学生被双向教师监督导致的时序信息错配问题，在短/长视频生成基准和时间变化相机控制方面达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式自回归视频生成需要低延迟生成和精确在线控制。现有的视频分布匹配蒸馏（DMD）流程使用双向教师对完整视频片段评分，教师得分可能依赖学生生成目标时不可用的未来帧和控制信号，导致教师监督与学生因果信息集不对齐。这种信息边界错配会损害few-step学生模型的训练质量。

Method: CMD构建了一个完全因果的DMD框架：1）用因果教师替代双向全片段评分教师，评估每个目标时无法访问未来帧或控制；2）用同一因果教师初始化few-step学生，使教师训练、学生蒸馏和推理阶段保持一致的因果公式；3）Prefix Scoring将监督与学生实际生成的上下文（缓存的学生生成前缀）对齐；4）Prefix Corruption通过扰动训练早期不可靠前缀来稳定训练，同时保持目标-上下文对齐；5）自然扩展到逐帧/逐块生成、长视频蒸馏和相机条件蒸馏。

Result: 实验表明，CMD在短视频和长视频基准上均取得自回归方法中的最优聚合性能，并显著提升了时间变化相机控制的遵从度。

Conclusion: 通过统一教师训练、学生蒸馏和推理阶段的因果公式，并配合前缀评分与前缀扰动机制，CMD有效解决了DMD中教师监督信息边界错配问题，为交互式自回归视频生成提供了更优的蒸馏框架。

Abstract: Interactive autoregressive video generation demands both low-latency rollouts and precise online control. Few-step distillation accelerates generation by reducing denoising steps, while online control imposes a causal constraint: frames and blocks should depend on history and controls available during generation. Existing video distribution matching distillation (DMD) pipelines, however, often supervise causal few-step students using bidirectional teachers that score complete clips. The score for a target can therefore depend on future frames and controls that were unavailable when the student generated it, misaligning teacher supervision with the student's causal information set. We introduce Context-Matched Distillation (CMD), a causal DMD framework that aligns teacher supervision with the information available when each target is generated. CMD replaces bidirectional full-clip scoring with a causal teacher that evaluates each target without access to future frames or controls. The same causal teacher initializes the few-step student, establishing a consistent causal formulation across teacher training, student distillation, and inference. Beyond aligning the temporal information boundary, Prefix Scoring matches supervision to the student's realized rollout context by evaluating each target under the cached student-generated prefix that produced it. Prefix Corruption further stabilizes training by perturbing unreliable prefixes produced early in training while preserving this target-context alignment. With a simple causal formulation, CMD naturally extends to frame-wise and chunk-wise generation, long video distillation, and camera-conditioned distillation. Experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art aggregate performance among autoregressive methods on both short- and long-video benchmarks, together with substantially improved adherence to time-varying camera controls.

</details>


### [76] [StreamTTT: Reconciling Real-Time Perception and Long-Term Memory in Streaming VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13416)
*Joya Chen,Zeyun Zhong,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StreamTTT通过将长程历史写入在线更新的快速权重（fast weights）而非注意力上下文，在流式视频大语言模型中同时兼顾实时感知与长期记忆，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频大语言模型（VLMs）在实时感知和长期记忆之间存在根本性权衡：压缩上下文可提升当前场景感知但损害长程回忆。此前的做法只能二选一，无法兼顾两者。

Method: 提出StreamTTT，将长程历史写入注意力上下文之外的在线更新快速权重中，仅保留短的滑动键值缓存（KV cache）用于近期证据，从而缓解注意力稀释问题。在离线长视频QA和新构建的实时QA语料上联合训练。

Result: 在OVO-Bench上，StreamTTT-4B相比SimpleStream-4B在实时感知上提升1.4分，在回溯推理（backward tracing）上提升3.7分；在StreamingBench的RTVU子集上，与更大的SimpleStream-8B表现相当。

Conclusion: StreamTTT证明了将长期记忆外置于注意力上下文、以快速权重形式在线更新的设计可以有效化解实时感知与长程记忆之间的权衡，为流式视频理解提供了一个新的可行路线。

Abstract: Humans effortlessly perceive the present while remembering the past, yet streaming VLMs often trade off real-time perception against long-term memory. Prior work shows that shortening the context can sharpen current-scene perception at the expense of long-range recall. To reconcile these abilities, we introduce StreamTTT, which writes long-range history into online-updated fast weights outside the attention context. This leaves a short sliding key-value cache dedicated to recent evidence, mitigating attention dilution. We train StreamTTT jointly on offline long-video QA and a newly constructed real-time QA corpus. On OVO-Bench, StreamTTT-4B outperforms SimpleStream-4B by 1.4 points in real-time perception and 3.7 points in backward tracing. It also remains competitive with the larger SimpleStream-8B on the Real-Time Visual Understanding (RTVU) subset of StreamingBench. Our code will be released.

</details>


### [77] [Edit2TikZ: A Comprehensive and Challenging Benchmark for Scientific Figure Editing with TikZ](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13441)
*Zongyun Zhang,Jiacheng Ruan,Xian Gao,Ruizhu Zhou,Lingcheng Meng,Lining Hu,Ting Liu,Yuzhuo Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Edit2TikZ，一个包含 1,548 个高质量样本的科学图示编辑基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）通过生成可编译的 TikZ 代码来进行科学图示编辑的能力。基准涵盖真实与受控合成编辑、文本与视觉定位、多步编辑及步级标注，并构建了人类对齐的评估框架。评估 14 个主流 MLLM 后发现当前系统不可靠（专有模型编译成功率仅 75%），为此构建 TikZEditMix 混合训练集并对紧凑模型采用重建-再编辑的课程学习，将 Qwen3.5-4B 的编译成功率从 45.35% 提升至 83.40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 TikZ 基准主要关注图示重建与生成，缺乏对指令引导的科学图示编辑（即需同时恢复视觉结构、理解编辑请求、生成可编译代码并保持无关内容不变）的系统性评估，因此需要构建全面的编辑基准来填补这一空白。

Method: 构建 Edit2TikZ 基准（含 1,548 个样本，融合真实与受控合成编辑案例，支持文本与视觉定位请求，包含多步编辑及步级标注）；设计人类对齐的评估框架衡量编辑完成度与无关内容保留度；构建 TikZEditMix 混合训练集，对紧凑模型采用重建-然后-编辑（reconstruction-then-editing）课程学习策略。

Result: 评估 14 个 MLLM 显示当前系统不可靠：专有模型平均编译成功率仅 75%，图示恢复与编辑正确性均受限；<9B 紧凑模型在指令遵循与完整图示生成上表现更差。采用训练策略后，Qwen3.5-4B 的编译成功率从 45.35% 提升至 83.40%，提出的评估指标平均提升 18.7 分。

Conclusion: Edit2TikZ 揭示了当前 MLLM 在科学图示编辑这一复杂任务上的显著不足，并验证了混合训练数据与重建-再编辑课程学习能大幅提升紧凑模型在该任务上的表现，为后续研究提供了基准与方法论基础。

Abstract: Although multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown substantial potential in visual understanding and graphic code generation, editing scientific figures through code presents a greater challenge: a model must jointly recover visual structure, ground the requested change, generate compilable code, and preserve all unrelated content. While existing TikZ benchmarks mainly focus on figure reconstruction and generation, few systematically evaluate instruction-guided scientific figure editing with compilable code. We introduce Edit2TikZ, a comprehensive benchmark for scientific figure editing tasks, featuring 1,548 diverse and high-quality samples. Edit2TikZ combines real-world and controlled synthetic edit cases, supports both textual and visual localization request, and contains multi-step editing, each with step-level annotations. We further construct a human-aligned evaluation framework to measure whether a requested edit is completed while irrelevant content is preserved. Utilizing Edit2TikZ, we evaluate 14 mainstream MLLMs and find that current systems remain unreliable: on average, proprietary models achieve a compilation success rate of merely 75% and remain limited in both figure restoration and edit correctness, while compact models below 9B struggle further with instruction following and complete figure generation. Therefore, we build a mixed training set TikZEditMix and adopt reconstruction-then-editing curriculum learning for compact models. On Qwen3.5-4B, this training improves the compilation success rate from 45.35% to 83.40% and yields an average improvement of 18.7 points across our proposed evaluation metrics. The code and data will be released at https://github.com/Solunny/Edit2TikZ.

</details>


### [78] [UniTexture: Cross-Task Universal Adversarial Textures for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13453)
*Yukun Dai,Mingzhe Dai,Tianshi Wang,Fengling Li,Jingjing Li,Lei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UniTexture，一种跨任务通用的对抗性纹理攻击方法，通过单个3D对象的表面纹理同时诱导多任务VLA（视觉-语言-动作）模型产生目标导向的动作偏差，并在OpenVLA和π_0.5上实现了攻击成功率的大幅降低和跨套件、跨模型的迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对机器人策略的攻击通常针对单一任务或指令优化，未能探索多任务VLA模型在跨任务维度上的脆弱性。随着VLA模型被赋予对具身智能体的直接控制权，其潜在的安全性风险亟需系统研究，特别是在跨任务层面上是否存在可利用的共享弱点。

Method: UniTexture通过可微分渲染器将策略动作输出的梯度反向传播到表面纹理参数上，对任务、指令、状态和视角的分布进行联合优化，使用目标导向的动作空间损失函数，共享单一纹理即可跨多个任务诱导攻击者定义的目标动作，而无需为每个任务单独优化纹理。

Result: 在OpenVLA和π_0.5上进行多任务操控评估，UniTexture将平均任务成功率从良性条件下的90.0%降至攻击下的48.4%，诱导了目标对齐的动作偏移，并展现出无需重新优化的跨套件和跨模型迁移能力。

Conclusion: 这些发现揭示了多任务VLA模型中存在可被单个对抗性表面纹理系统性利用的共享跨任务脆弱性，表明当前VLA模型在安全性方面仍有显著不足，需要进一步加强对对抗性物理干扰的防御研究。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as generalist robotic policies capable of following diverse language instructions and performing a wide range of manipulation tasks. However, their direct control over embodied agents also exposes them to adversarial interference that may cause unsafe physical behaviors. Existing attacks on robotic policies are typically optimized for a single task or instruction, leaving the cross-task vulnerabilities of multitask VLAs largely unexplored. We introduce UniTexture, a cross-task universal adversarial texture attack that uses a single textured 3D object to induce targeted deviations in VLA action predictions across multiple tasks. UniTexture backpropagates gradients from the policy's action outputs to surface texture parameters through a differentiable renderer. It jointly optimizes the shared texture over a distribution of tasks, instructions, states, and viewpoints using a targeted action-space objective, steering predicted actions toward attacker-defined targets without optimizing a separate texture for each task. We evaluate UniTexture on OpenVLA and $π_{0.5}$ across diverse manipulation tasks and multiple evaluation settings. UniTexture reduces the mean task success rate from 90.0% under benign conditions to 48.4% under attack, induces target-aligned action shifts, and further exhibits cross-suite and cross-model transfer without re-optimization. Together, these findings reveal shared cross-task vulnerabilities in multitask VLAs that can be systematically exploited through a single adversarial surface texture.

</details>


### [79] [Evaluation of Clinically Steerable Retinal Image Generation from Foundation Model Latent Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13455)
*Zuzanna A. Wakefield-Skórniewska,Bartłomiej W. Papież*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估了四种视网膜基础模型在表征分词器框架下的可控图像生成能力，发现生成的表征和图像能忠实继承表型信息且在原始基础模型评估中优于传统潜扩散模型，但用真实图像训练的分类器评估时优势消失，揭示了合成-真实表征差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学基础模型能学习临床有意义表型的潜在表征，但其支持可控图像生成的能力尚未被充分探索。理解基础模型潜空间能否保真地传递人口统计学和临床信息到合成图像，对医学图像生成的下游应用至关重要。

Method: 在表征分词器框架中评估四种视网膜基础模型，通过多个下游预测任务比较生成表征和图像与常规潜扩散方法的性能差异，并同时使用原始基础模型和基于真实图像训练的独立分类器进行双向评估。

Result: 生成的表征和图像在所属基础模型内部评估时能忠实继承表型信息，并一致优于常规潜扩散方法；但当使用基于真实图像训练的分类器评估时，这些优势基本消失，暴露出此前未被描述的合成-真实表征差距。

Conclusion: 基础模型潜空间为可控视网膜合成提供了强力支撑，但需要更好地将合成表征与真实图像分布对齐，以弥合合成-真实差距。

Abstract: Medical foundation models learn latent representations of clinically meaningful phenotypes, yet their ability to support controllable image generation remains largely unexplored. We evaluate four retinal foundation models within the representation tokenizer framework and examine whether demographic and clinical information encoded in latent representations from foundation models is preserved during synthetic image generation. We show that generated representations and images faithfully inherit phenotype information when evaluated within their originating foundation models, consistently outperforming conventional latent diffusion on multiple downstream prediction tasks. However, these gains largely disappear when evaluated using classifiers trained on real images, revealing a previously uncharacterised synthetic-to-real representation gap. These findings demonstrate that foundation-model latent spaces provide a powerful substrate for controllable retinal synthesis while highlighting the need to better align synthetic representations with real-image distributions.

</details>


### [80] [Fine-Grained Action Recognition with Cross-Attentive Latent Sparse Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13458)
*Imtiaz Ul Hassan,Tasweer Ahmad,Nik Bessis,Ardhendu Behera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FineX是一种用于细粒度人类动作识别（FHAR）的框架，它将细粒度线索分解为RGB外观、姿态热图几何和骨骼图拓扑三种模态，通过成对交叉注意力进行模态间信息交换，并采用流式潜在稀疏混合专家（MoE）对每种表示进行条件路由和精炼。在Gym99、Gym288和Diving48上达到SOTA，在长尾Gym288上平均类别准确率从68.6%提升至76.2%（+7.6点），无需文本监督或大规模视觉-语言预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度人类动作识别需要区分在身体构型、时序或局部外观上高度相似的动作。RGB表示保留视觉上下文但抑制了关节级几何信息，而骨骼表示编码运动学却丢弃了密集空间细节。现有单一模态方法难以同时捕获这两类互补线索，因此需要一种能够有效融合RGB外观与骨骼几何的结构化方法。

Method: FineX包含三个关键组件：(1) 将细粒度线索分解为RGB外观、姿态热图几何和骨骼图拓扑三种互补表示；(2) 采用成对交叉注意力机制实现对称的、保持流（stream-preserving）的模态间信息交换；(3) 引入流式潜在稀疏混合专家（streamwise latent sparse Mixture-of-Experts），将每个表示路由到与内容相关的共享专家子集，并用负载均衡目标进行正则化。

Result: FineX在Gym99、Gym288和Diving48三个基准上均取得最先进结果。尤其在长尾分布的Gym288上，将平均类别准确率从68.6%提升至76.2%（提升7.6个百分点），且未依赖文本监督或大规模视觉-语言预训练。

Conclusion: 结构化视觉-姿态-图融合（structured visual-pose-graph fusion）与条件专家精炼（conditional expert refinement）的结合对细粒度人类动作识别具有显著优势，证明了无需额外语言监督即可通过多模态结构融合有效提升FHAR性能。

Abstract: Fine-grained human action recognition (FHAR) must distinguish visually similar actions that differ mainly in body configuration, timing, or local appearance. RGB representations retain visual context but often suppress joint-level geometry, whereas skeleton representations encode kinematics but discard dense spatial detail. We introduce FineX, which factorizes fine-grained cues into RGB appearance, pose heatmap geometry, and skeletal-graph topology. Pairwise cross-attention enables symmetric, stream-preserving information exchange, followed by a streamwise latent sparse Mixture-of-Experts that routes each representation to a content-dependent subset of shared experts, regularized by a load-balancing objective. FineX achieves state-of-the-art results on Gym99, Gym288, and Diving48. On the long-tailed Gym288, it raises mean class accuracy from 68.6% to 76.2% (+7.6 points) without textual supervision or large-scale vision-language pre-training, demonstrating the benefit of structured visual-pose-graph fusion and conditional expert refinement for FHAR.

</details>


### [81] [SNM-VFI: Symmetric Nonlinear Motion-Guided Generative Video Frame Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13460)
*Jisoo Jeong,Hong Cai,Jamie Menjay Lin,Hanno Ackermann,Hyeonjun Sim,Yinhao Zhu,Yunxiao Shi,Fatih Porikli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SNM-VFI是一种无需训练的框架，结合预训练光流模型和视频扩散模型，通过对称非线性运动模型生成对应感知的中间帧，实现运动可控的生成式视频帧插值，在DAVIS、Sintel和KITTI基准上达到优异的感知质量、重建精度和时间一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于扩散的VFI方法从随机噪声合成中间帧，难以保持稠密运动对应关系，且缺乏对生成过程的运动控制能力。

Method: 首先利用预训练光流模型构建多帧非线性流引导的中间帧和置信图；将这些流引导帧编码为潜在先验，用于初始化和迭代引导预训练视频扩散模型的生成过程；最后使用置信图将结构可靠的流预测与扩散生成细节（如遮挡和物体边界区域）进行融合。

Result: 在DAVIS、Sintel和KITTI等挑战性基准上，SNM-VFI实现了强感知质量、有竞争力的重建精度以及跨多样化运动场景的稳健时间一致性。

Conclusion: SNM-VFI验证了通过对称非线性运动模型结合光流先验与视频扩散模型，可以在无需训练的情况下实现高质量的运动可控视频帧插值，为生成式VFI提供了新的思路。

Abstract: We propose Symmetric Nonlinear Motion-guided Generative Video Frame Interpolation (SNM-VFI), a training-free framework for motion-controllable generative video frame interpolation with pre-trained optical flow and video diffusion models. Unlike conventional diffusion-based VFI methods that synthesize intermediate frames from random noise, SNM-VFI guides the generative process with correspondence-aware frames produced by a symmetric nonlinear motion model. Specifically, we first utilize a pre-trained optical flow model to construct multi-frame nonlinear flow-based intermediate frames and confidence maps. These flow-guided frames are then encoded as latent priors to initialize and iteratively guide a pre-trained Video Diffusion model, enabling the diffusion model to preserve dense motion correspondence while improving perceptual realism. To further enhance output quality, we employ confidence maps to fuse structurally reliable flow-based predictions with diffusion-generated details in uncertain regions such as occlusions and object boundaries. Extensive evaluations on challenging benchmarks, including DAVIS, Sintel, and KITTI, demonstrate that SNM-VFI achieves strong perceptual quality, competitive reconstruction accuracy, and robust temporal coherence across diverse motion scenarios.

</details>


### [82] [MLLM-Routed Heterogeneous Ensembles for Robust Cross-Dataset Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13463)
*Daniel Perkins,John Squires,Janou Milligan,Chandra Raskoti,Linda Ungerboeck*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ARMDIL是一个基于多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的动态路由框架，通过集成多种视觉骨干网络（CNN、自监督表示学习模型和视觉语言模型）实现跨域图像分类。该框架利用MLLM代理为每张图像选择最合适的视觉骨干，并通过简单的提示词修改实现新信息的无缝集成，同时通过自然语言推理轨迹提升可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图像分类模型通常在单一任务特定数据集上表现出色，但在跨领域和跨难度级别的泛化能力上存在明显不足。作者旨在解决这一问题，构建一个能够动态适应不同视觉域并保持高性能的通用视觉系统。

Method: ARMDIL采用集成学习策略，包含三种不同类型的视觉骨干网络：卷积神经网络（ResNets）、自监督表示学习模型（SSL）和视觉语言模型（VLMs）。所有模型在由多个不同分布和特征的数据集构建的统一标签空间上进行训练。一个多模态大语言模型代理负责将每张输入图像动态路由到最合适的视觉骨干网络。

Result: 实验评估揭示了各架构在不同视觉域中的独特能力和脆弱性。ARMDIL能够有效权衡这些优缺点，其性能可与专门的基于训练的路由器相媲美。此外，ARMDIL通过简单的提示词修改即可集成新信息，显著提升了适应性，并通过自然语言推理轨迹增强了可解释性。

Conclusion: ARMDIL在跨数据集图像分类方面取得了实质性进展，为构建更可靠的通用视觉系统（如AI助手和自主机器人）铺平了道路，在性能、适应性和可解释性三个维度均展现出显著优势。

Abstract: Modern image classification models excel when trained on single task-specific datasets but often struggle to generalize across domains and difficulty levels. We propose ARMDIL, an Adaptive Router for Multi-Domain Image classification with LLMs. ARMDIL is an ensemble that uses a multimodal large language model (MLLM) agent to dynamically route each image to the most suitable vision backbone. Our diverse ensemble employs convolutional neural networks (ResNets), self-supervised representation learners (SSL), and vision-language models (VLMs), each trained on a unified label space constructed from multiple image datasets with differing distributions and characteristics. Empirical evaluations illuminate the distinct capabilities and vulnerabilities of each architecture across disparate visual domains. Crucially, we show that ARMDIL effectively navigates these trade-offs, performing competitively with specialized training-based routers. Furthermore, it drastically improves adaptability by allowing new information to be integrated via simple prompt modifications, while enhancing interpretability through natural language reasoning traces. These advances in cross-dataset image classification pave the way for more reliable general-purpose vision systems such as AI assistants and autonomous robots.

</details>


### [83] [MapRoute++: Surrogate-Guided Semantic Routing for Visual Concept Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13478)
*Ashok Urlana,L. D. M. S. Sai Teja,Vivek Hruday Kavuri,Ponnurangam Kumaraguru*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提交至Genμ 2.0挑战赛任务3（视觉概念遗忘），基于MapRoute方法，引入任务特定训练目标、更丰富的概念表征和语义路由来实现概念特定的映射器选择，在稳定扩散v1.4上以ERR指标平均超越SOTA基线12.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉概念遗忘是生成模型中重要的安全与隐私需求，现有方法在去除目标概念时往往会损害无关概念或语义相邻概念。本文旨在解决这一鲁棒性-保留性-安全性之间的平衡问题。

Method: 在MapRoute基础上提出三项改进：(1)引入任务特定的训练目标；(2)构建更丰富的概念表征；(3)采用语义路由实现概念特定的映射器选择，使每个概念都能被精准且有针对性地遗忘。

Result: 在Stable Diffusion v1.4上使用Erasing-Retention-Robustness (ERR)指标评估，跨越五个概念类别平均超越当前最先进基线12.1%，取得了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 该方法有效改善了概念遗忘的鲁棒性，在去除目标概念的同时很好地保留了无关概念和语义相邻概念，证明了任务特定目标和语义路由策略在视觉概念遗忘任务中的有效性。

Abstract: We present our submission to Task 3 of the Gen$μ$ 2.0 Challenge on visual concept unlearning. Building on MapRoute, we introduce task-specific training objectives, richer concept representations, and semantic routing for concept-specific mapper selection. Our approach improves robust concept removal while preserving unrelated and semantically adjacent concepts. On the official benchmark, evaluated using the Erasing-Retention-Robustness (ERR) metric on Stable Diffusion v1.4, our method outperforms the state-of-the-art baseline by 12.1\% on average across the five concept categories, achieving substantial gains.

</details>


### [84] [TraVEL: Trajectory-Guided Video Embedding Learning for Driving-Video Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13495)
*Yi-Chung Chen,Philip Jacobson,Tom Lampo,Yiren Lu,Jin Yao,David I. Inouye,Jing Gao,Danhua Guo,Burhan Yaman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TraVEL框架，通过将自车轨迹相似度作为强化学习奖励（GRPO），对通用多模态嵌入模型进行运动感知微调，从而提升驾驶视频检索中运动中心事件（如左右转弯、加减速）的区分能力，而推理时仍只需单向量视频嵌入，无需轨迹、专家规则或辅助感知输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模驾驶日志中高效检索相关片段对数据整理、模型开发和安全性分析至关重要。基于规则的结构化检索系统虽然能显式定位驾驶事件，但需要专家定义规则、辅助数据和多阶段感知管道。多模态嵌入模型虽然更简洁高效，但通用模型常依赖静态场景上下文捷径，难以区分运动中心事件（如左转vs右转、加速vs减速）。

Method: 分两阶段进行：(1) 用InfoNCE目标在nuReasoning的成对片段和推理轨迹上微调Qwen3-VL-Embedding；(2) 提出TraVEL（Trajectory-Guided Video Embedding Learning）运动感知微调框架，将自车轨迹相似度作为Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO）的奖励。轨迹仅作为训练时的特权监督信号，推理时仍只用单向量视频嵌入，不依赖自车位姿、专家规则或辅助感知输出。同时从nuReasoning构建了驾驶视频检索基准。

Result: TraVEL在多个模型规模上提升了运动中心检索性能：相对于SFT，在2B规模上纵向和横向mAP分别提升9.8和4.7个点；在8B规模上分别提升7.2和1.5个点。

Conclusion: TraVEL将物理接地监督（轨迹）与高效的基于嵌入的检索相结合，在训练阶段利用特权轨迹信息增强运动理解，同时保持推理时的轻量级单向量检索方案，显著改善驾驶视频中细粒度运动事件的检索能力。

Abstract: Efficiently retrieving relevant clips from large-scale driving logs is essential for data curation, model development, and safety analysis. Structured and rule-based retrieval systems can explicitly target driving events, but typically require expert-defined rules, auxiliary data, and multi-stage perception pipelines. Multimodal embedding models offer a simpler and more efficient alternative by representing each video with a single searchable vector. However, general-purpose models often rely on shortcuts from static scene context and struggle to distinguish motion-centric events, such as turning left versus right or accelerating versus decelerating. In this work, we study how to adapt a general-purpose multimodal embedding model to driving-video retrieval. We first fine-tune Qwen3-VL-Embedding on paired clips and reasoning traces from nuReasoning using an InfoNCE objective. While this stage substantially improves overall retrieval, caption supervision alone remains insufficient for fine-grained motion understanding. We therefore introduce TraVEL (Trajectory-Guided Video Embedding Learning), a motion-aware fine-tuning framework that uses ego-trajectory similarity as a reward within Group Relative Policy Optimization. Trajectories serve only as privileged training supervision; retrieval still operates on single-vector video embeddings without ego poses, expert rules, or auxiliary perception outputs. We further construct a driving-video retrieval benchmark from nuReasoning. Experiments show that TraVEL improves motion-centric retrieval across model scales: relative to SFT, it raises longitudinal and lateral mAP by 9.8 and 4.7 points at 2B, with corresponding gains of 7.2 and 1.5 points at 8B. TraVEL thus combines physically grounded supervision with efficient embedding-based search.

</details>


### [85] [GS$^{2}$CI: Robust Gaussian Splatting For Snapshot Compressive Imaging via Large Vision Model Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13502)
*Yanming Yang,Chenxi Song,Ping Wang,Xin Yuan,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个从单次快照压缩成像（SCI）测量中重建高质量3D场景的新框架，结合3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）和视觉基础模型（VFM）的先验知识，通过3D VFM初始化、2D VFM伪视图监督以及SCI特定的高斯致密化策略（OSGR）实现领先的3D重建性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于SCI的3D重建方法面临信息损失、视角多样性有限以及联合优化3D表示与相机位姿的计算负担等显著挑战，亟需更高效且鲁棒的重建方案。

Method: 1) 利用3D VFM从SCI测量中初始化重建，并结合SCI感知的高斯优化；2) 粗阶段收敛后，使用2D VFM在合成视点提供伪视图监督以细化局部外观；3) 提出Opacity-Guided Splitting and Growth Regulation (OSGR)策略，通过局部不透明度统计增强分裂候选，以平均不透明度正则化抑制补偿性不透明度膨胀，并限制表示增长。

Result: 在多个基准上的大量实验表明，该方法在重建质量和视角变化鲁棒性上达到最优综合性能，同时保持竞争力的计算效率。

Conclusion: 通过将3DGS与大规模视觉基础模型先验相结合，并引入SCI特定的致密化正则化策略，可以有效地从单次SCI测量中重建高质量3D场景，为解决SCI单次测量下3D重建难题提供了新路径。

Abstract: Snapshot Compressive Imaging (SCI) offers an efficient solution for high-speed video acquisition and, under exposure-time camera--scene relative motion, multi-view scene capture by compressing temporal or spatial information into a single 2D measurement. While recent studies have explored SCI for 3D scene reconstruction, existing methods struggle with significant challenges due to information loss, limited viewpoint diversity, and the computational burden of jointly optimizing 3D representations and camera poses. In this work, we propose a novel framework that reconstructs high-quality 3D scenes from a single SCI measurement by leveraging 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) and the powerful priors of large-scale vision foundation models (VFMs). Our primary reconstruction combines measurement-derived 3D VFM initialization with SCI-aware Gaussian optimization. After coarse-stage convergence, an auxiliary 2D VFM provides pseudo-view supervision at synthesized viewpoints for local appearance refinement. To further address the instability caused by ambiguous SCI supervision during 3DGS optimization, we introduce Opacity-Guided Splitting and Growth Regulation (OSGR), an SCI-specific densification strategy that augments split candidates using local opacity statistics, discourages loss-compensating opacity inflation through mean-opacity regulation, and bounds representation growth with explicit candidate-ratio and Gaussian-count constraints. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves the strongest overall performance, combining leading reconstruction quality and robustness to viewpoint variation with competitive computational efficiency.

</details>


### [86] [TabSOM: A tabular-to-image encoding method based on self-organizing maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13513)
*David Chushig-Muzo,María Ángeles Rodríguez de Cara,Eva Milara,Francisco J. Lara-Abelenda,Luis Zhinin-Vera,Diego H. Peluffo-Ordóñez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TabSOM是一种基于自组织映射（SOM）的表格数据到图像编码方法，通过匈牙利算法将每个特征分配到固定画布位置，并利用SOM分量平面构建特征关系图，生成多尺度节点通道的图像表示。在12种现有方法对比中，TabSOM在所有数据集上排名第一或第二，方差最低，并通过原型启发部分依赖图和类别分离重要性分数提供可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的表格转图像方法（如基于t-SNE、UMAP、PCA的映射）仅编码每个特征的边缘值，丢弃了特征之间的相互关系信息。作者希望设计一种新的编码方式，既能保留特征空间布局，又能捕获特征间的成对关系，从而提升深度学习模型在表格数据上的预测性能和可解释性。

Method: TabSOM基于自组织映射构建：(i) 利用无碰撞的匈牙利分配（Hungarian assignment）从分量平面推导出每个输入特征的固定画布位置；(ii) 从SOM分量平面提取捕获特征间成对关系的图结构。生成的图像堆叠两种多尺度节点通道：一种以固定尺度编码特征值，另一种将特征间成对交互编码为相关特征之间的空间连接。此外提出两种SOM派生可解释性方法：原型启发的部分依赖图和类别分离重要性分数。

Result: 在公开二分类数据集上与12种现有表格转图像方法对比，TabSOM在每一个数据集上排名第一或第二，且在所有评估方法中方差最低。可解释性结果经Random Forest、XGBoost和SHAP验证，类别分离分数在排名靠前的特征上与既有基线合理吻合，同时捕获了来自输入数据的互补结构信息。

Conclusion: TabSOM提供了一种有效且可解释的方法，将深度学习架构应用于表格数据，弥补了该领域中性能与可解释性之间的差距。

Abstract: Tabular-to-image methods have emerged as novel approaches to leverage the high predictive performance of convolutional neural networks and vision transformers. They convert tabular data into image representations, mapping each feature at a fixed pixel location derived from a dimensionality-reduction method (e.g., t-SNE, UMAP, PCA). However, they encode only the marginal value of each feature and discard information about feature relationships. We propose TabSOM, a tabular-to-image encoding built on the Self-Organizing Map (SOM), which provides: (i) a spatial layout in which every input feature occupies a fixed canvas position derived from its component plane via collision-free Hungarian assignment; and (ii) a graph that captures pairwise feature relationships derived from the SOM component planes. The resulting image stacks two multi-scale node channels: one encodes feature values at fixed scales, while the other encodes pairwise feature interactions as spatial connections between related features. Two SOM-derived interpretability approaches are introduced: a prototype-inspired partial dependence plot and a class--separation importance score. Benchmarked against twelve existing tabular-to-image methods across public binary-classification datasets, TabSOM ranks first or second on every dataset and achieves the lowest variance of any method evaluated. Interpretability obtained with TabSOM was validated against Random Forest, XGBoost, and SHAP, the class-separation score shows reasonable agreement with established baselines on the top-ranked features while capturing complementary structural information from input data. These results demonstrate that TabSOM provides an effective and interpretable approach for applying deep learning architectures to tabular data, bridging the performance--interpretability gap in this domain.

</details>


### [87] [SCULPT: Subtractive Composition for 3D Part Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13541)
*Sikuang Li,Chen Yang,Jiemin Fang,Jiazhong Cen,Yuhe Wei,Jichen Pang,Wei Shen,Qi Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SCULPT提出了一种通过减法组合（subtractive composition）进行部件感知3D生成的新框架，在结构化3D潜空间中迭代地联合预测"提取部件+剩余物体"，通过耦合去噪过程同步生成两者而非事后拼接，从而避免部件间缝隙、互穿和材质不连续等问题。实验在PartObjaverse上达到SOTA几何效果，并保持组装后完整的物体重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有部件感知3D生成方法存在根本性缺陷：基于分割的方法在完整形状生成后才划分部件边界，导致部件语义被先验锁定；基于加法合成的方法从预定义布局/框/标记生成部件再拼接，共享边界容易出现缝隙、互穿和材质不连续。二者都未在生成循环内部原生地处理部件结构。

Method: SCULPT采用减法组合策略：在结构化3D潜空间中，给定完整物体表示，通过联合分割预测器（joint split predictor）迭代地生成一个提取部件和剩余物体。预测器执行耦合去噪过程，同时以图像和当前3D状态为条件，使两个输出在原生稀疏3D支撑的并集上处理，允许相邻支撑重叠而非强制不相交的体素划分。滚动过程在剩余支撑为空或达到安全上限时终止，使部件数量可根据物体自适应。

Result: 在PartObjaverse基准上取得了SOTA几何结果，同时保持部件组装后完整物体的强重建质量。在四个数据集图像、一个文本到图像生成输入和一个真实世界照片上的结果展示了超越基准的细粒度纹理部件分解能力。

Conclusion: SCULPT通过减法组合将部件结构纳入生成过程本身，而非事后处理，有效解决了现有分割法和加法法的核心缺陷，在保持整体生成质量的同时实现了自适应、细粒度的部件分解，为部件感知3D生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: Part-aware 3D generation aims to create digital assets that are coherent as complete objects while exposing structural parts for editing, material assignment, animation, and reuse. Existing methods impose this structure outside the native generation loop: segmentation-based methods partition an already generated shape, while additive methods synthesize parts from predefined layouts, boxes, or tokens and then reconcile them into a whole. The former preserves the generated geometry but fixes the object before part boundaries are determined; the latter exposes part cardinality but often leaves shared boundaries vulnerable to gaps, interpenetrations, and material discontinuities. In this paper, we propose SCULPT, a framework that addresses these challenges through subtractive composition. Given a complete object represented in a structured 3D latent space, SCULPT iteratively applies a joint split predictor to generate one extracted part together with the remaining object. The predictor performs a coupled denoising process conditioned on both the image and the current 3D state, so the extracted part and updated remainder are generated together rather than reconciled after generation. The joint split predictor processes both outputs on the union of their native sparse 3D supports, allowing neighboring supports to overlap rather than imposing a disjoint voxel partition. The rollout ends when the remainder support becomes empty or reaches a fixed safety cap, allowing the number of generated parts to adapt to each object within that bound. Extensive experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art geometry on PartObjaverse while preserving strong complete-object reconstruction after part assembly. Results on four dataset images, one text-to-image-generated input, and one real-world photograph further show fine-grained textured part decomposition beyond the benchmark.

</details>


### [88] [Alaya-EVOKE: From Linear-Scaling Supervision to Endless World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13546)
*Yuanyang Yin,Gongxuan Wang,Yifan Zhan,Chuanhao Li,Kaipeng Zhang,Feng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Evoke 提出了一种交互式世界模型，通过将场景几何外部化到相机索引的世界状态库中，并重新设计教师模型用于长时程监督，以解决持久记忆、响应式交互和长时程生成之间的冲突。其学生模型仅需三步生成且无需 classifier-free guidance，在单张 H200 上每 1.5 秒块仅需 2.11 秒生成，在 WBench 上达到 SOTA，并在 VBench-Long 和 VBench-2.0 上保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式世界模型面临三重需求冲突：维持历史记忆（denoiser 上下文或 KV 缓存）会随会话长度增长而成本增加；低延迟交互依赖少步生成但受限于教师能力；现有方法无法同时兼顾持久记忆、响应式控制和长时程一致性。作者希望打破这些权衡。

Method: （1）外部化持久世界状态：场景几何存储在外部的相机索引世界状态库中，仅检索与当前视图相关的信息，使 denoiser 上下文随会话增长保持有界。（2）重新设计教师用于长时程监督：教师采用稀疏注意力，包括块状分组、对选定的远距离帧检索以及线性注意力的全局状态，使内存和计算线性增长。（3）每块条件化实现提示变更和事件控制。（4）30秒分布匹配目标在自强迫 rollout 下应用，将长时程能力迁移到三步学生模型（不使用 classifier-free guidance）。

Result: 在单张 H200 上以 384×640 分辨率，每 1.5 秒 chunk 生成耗时 2.11 秒。作为三步世界模型，在 WBench 上达到最先进性能，在 VBench-Long 和 VBench-2.0 上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过外部递归记忆和有界上下文，Evoke 实现了开放式的、持续演化的交互式生成，有效解决了长时程漂移问题，同时保持了响应式的条件控制能力。

Abstract: Interactive world models must support persistent memory, responsive interaction, and long-horizon generation, yet these requirements place conflicting demands on the model. Maintaining history in the denoiser context or key-value cache incurs growing cost, forcing a trade-off between session length and retained memory, while low-latency interaction relies on few-step generation whose capabilities are bounded by its teacher. Evoke addresses both limitations by externalizing persistent world state and redesigning the teacher for long-horizon interactive generation. Scene geometry is maintained in an external, camera-indexed world state bank, from which only view-relevant information is retrieved, keeping the denoiser context bounded as the session grows. Rather than treating the teacher as a fixed generator, we design it for long-horizon supervision: its sparse attention combines chunk-wise grouping, retrieval of selected distant frames, and a linear-attention global state, yielding linear growth in memory and compute while enabling supervision over long horizons. Such supervision exposes content drift that stays locally plausible within short windows, while per-chunk conditioning enables prompt changes and event control throughout the sequence. A 30-second distribution-matching objective, applied under self-forced rollouts, transfers both capabilities to a three-step student that uses no classifier-free guidance, improving resistance to long-term drift while preserving responsive conditioning. With bounded context and recurrent external memory, Evoke supports open-ended, continuously evolving generation; on a single H200 at $384\times 640$, each $1.5\,\mathrm{s}$ chunk is generated in $2.11\,\mathrm{s}$. As a three-step world model, Evoke achieves state-of-the-art performance on WBench while remaining competitive on VBench-Long and VBench-2.0.

</details>


### [89] [PlayWorld: Benchmarking World Models with Agent Players over Long-Horizon Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13552)
*Kaixin Ding,Xi Chen,Minghong Cai,Zhiyuan Xu,Yiyang Wang,Yuxiang Lu,Junyi Li,Shuyang Chen,Yuan Gao,Xin Tao,Pengfei Wan,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PlayWorld基准，利用多模态Agent Players与视频世界模型互动以完成指定的长时程目标，从而在跨模型间实现公平比较，评估几何一致性、交互保真度、视野外演化与洞察演化四个核心维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有固定动作条件下的评估难以公平比较不同视频世界模型，因为不同模型达成同一目标所需的动作序列可能差异很大，需要一种基于目标的互动式评估范式。

Method: 采用多模态Agent Players作为交互代理，使其与世界模型互动以追求给定的长时程目标；构建PlayWorld基准，包含171个场景和指定目标；从几何一致性、交互保真度、视野外演化、洞察演化四个核心维度以及视频质量和可控性等基础能力维度进行评估。

Result: 在九个最先进的视频世界模型上进行实验，发现当前模型在长时程交互目标上仍不可靠，尤其是在保持空间一致性和持续状态演化方面存在明显不足。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于Agent Player的互动式评估范式，弥补了传统固定动作评估的不足；实验揭示现有世界模型在长时程交互和空间一致性方面仍存在显著短板，为未来研究指明改进方向，并开源了代码和数据。

Abstract: Video world models simulate future states conditioned on current observations and user actions. Recent systems have demonstrated impressive video consistency and action controllability over long sequences. However, fairly comparing these interactive models remains challenging. In practice, a human player typically evaluates a world model by pursuing long-horizon objectives through interaction. For example, a user may turn around 360 degrees to see whether the environment remains consistent, or walk into the water and inspect whether realistic water ripples are generated. The action sequence required to achieve the same objective may vary substantially between models, making fixed action-conditioned evaluation unsuitable for cross-model comparison. To address this, we employ multi-modal Agent Players to interact with world models toward specified long-horizon objectives. Building on this paradigm, we introduce PlayWorld, a benchmark providing 171 scenarios, each with a specified objective. To evaluate performance thoroughly, we assess models along four core dimensions: geometry consistency, interaction fidelity, out-of-sight evolution, and insight evolution. In addition, we incorporate basic ability metrics for video quality and controllability. Experiments across nine state-of-the-art world models reveal that current models remain unreliable on long-horizon interactive objectives, particularly in maintaining spatial consistency and persistent state evolution. Code and data are available at https://github.com/kxding/PlayWorld.

</details>


### [90] [V-RAE: Rethinking Video Latent Spaces for Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13556)
*Minghui Guo,Shengqiong Wu,Hao Fei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出V-RAE，一种在冻结的视觉基础模型表示之上构建紧致生成潜空间的视频表示自编码器，通过轻量级时序池化模块去除时序冗余并保留语义结构，实验表明其在视频重建、语义探测和类条件生成任务上均优于现有大规模预训练视频VAE，同时提出tFVD作为与时序一致性和下游生成质量更相关的诊断指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频自编码器的潜空间主要针对像素级重建进行优化，缺乏高层语义组织，而重建最优的潜空间未必适合生成建模。作者旨在将冻结的视觉基础模型的高层语义表示引入视频潜空间，以构建对生成任务更友好的紧凑表示。

Method: V-RAE由两部分组成：(1) 在冻结的视觉基础模型表示之上构建紧凑生成潜空间；(2) 轻量级时序池化模块用于去除时序冗余同时保留语义结构；(3) 视频解码器从压缩特征中重建连续运动。此外提出tFVD（temporal-coherence diagnostic）指标用于更可靠地评估下游生成质量。

Result: V-RAE在K600上达到2.13 rFVD，优于所有被评估的大规模预训练视频VAE；潜空间保留了远超传统视频tokenizer潜空间的语义信息；在匹配生成条件下，最佳变体在UCF101和K600上分别达到117.86和19.16的gFVD分数，且收敛速度快达6倍；在Cityscapes未来的视频预测任务上也优于Wan 2.2 VAE潜空间。

Conclusion: 实验表明，重建质量单独不足以表征生成效用，冻结的语义表示能够同时支持视频重建、生成和预测建模，为视频生成领域提供了新的潜空间构建范式。

Abstract: Latent video generation relies on autoencoders to define a compact space in which generative models operate. Although video autoencoder architectures have evolved substantially, their latent spaces are still optimized primarily for pixel-level reconstruction and provide limited high-level semantic organization. A reconstruction-optimal latent space, however, need not be well suited to generative modeling. We propose V-RAE, a video representation autoencoder that builds compact generative latents on top of frozen vision foundation model representations. A lightweight temporal pooling module removes temporal redundancy while preserving semantic structure, and a video decoder reconstructs continuous motion from the compressed features. We evaluate V-RAE with four representative frozen encoders on video reconstruction, semantic probing, and class-conditional generation. V-RAE achieves 2.13 rFVD on K600, outperforming all evaluated large-scale pretrained video VAEs. Its latents retain substantially more semantic information than conventional video tokenizer latents. Under matched generation settings, our best variant achieves gFVD scores of 117.86 and 19.16 on UCF101 and K600, respectively, while converging up to 6x faster}. We further show that reconstruction quality alone is insufficient to characterize generative utility and introduce tFVD, a temporal-coherence diagnostic that correlates more reliably with downstream generation quality. Beyond video generation, V-RAE also improves future video prediction on Cityscapes over the Wan 2.2 VAE latent space under matched prediction settings. Taken together, the experiments show that frozen semantic representations can support video reconstruction, generation, and predictive modeling. The project page: https://v-rae.github.io/.

</details>


### [91] [AutoDesign: Meta-Harness Optimization for Long-Horizon Agentic Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13560)
*Yaxin Luo,Haobin Jiang,Jialv Zou,Xu Huang,Wenhao Yan,Haodong Li,Zhengrong Yue,Jing Li,Xiaofu Chen,Xiaohan Zhao,Jiacheng Liu,Jiacheng Cui,Zhiqiang Shen,Xiaotong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoDesign框架，将多模态媒体生成（如论文转海报）视为长周期智能体过程，通过元harness优化器引导代码智能体基于rollout反馈递归改进harness系统。在PosterBench基准上达到78.32分，超越商业系统Claude Design 7.45分，并在多配置下一致提升性能，且能以低成本（<3美元/40分钟）自主完成完整生成循环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的媒体生成harness系统是静态的，缺乏自我改进能力。理想的harness系统应当与人类设计先验保持一致，并通过经验积累和递归自我优化来持续改进，但现有范式无法实现这一目标。

Method: 提出AutoDesign框架：（1）采用元harness优化器指导代码智能体基于rollout反馈递归改进harness；（2）聚焦学术论文到海报生成任务；（3）发布PosterBench基准（100篇论文主轨+10篇论文子集用于受控评估）；（4）在7种受控代码智能体模型配置下进行系统评估，并开展全自主长周期循环实验和系统盲人研究。

Result: AutoDesign在PosterBench主轨上取得78.32分，超越Claude Design 7.45分。集成学习的DesignHarness在7种配置下平均将PosterBench Score从54.99提升至67.39（+12.4%）。全自主模式下40分钟内执行253次工具调用和11次编辑回合，成本低于3美元，达到平均会议海报质量。系统盲人研究中获得最高人工偏好。

Conclusion: 验证了将媒体生成建模为可递归自我改进的智能体harness过程的有效性，AutoDesign在性能、效率、成本和人工偏好方面均优于现有系统，为多模态生成任务的智能体化提供了新范式。

Abstract: Transforming multimodal sources into condensed and structured media outputs can be fundamentally conceptualized as a long-horizon agentic process centered on a model-harness system. While an ideal harness system should align with human design priors and accumulate reusable experience through empirical exploration to drive recursive self-improvement, existing paradigms remain static and fall short of this capability. In this paper, we present AutoDesign, a framework that aligns with human design priors, where a meta-harness optimizer guides a code agent to recursively improve harness based on rollout feedback. To instantiate and evaluate this framework, we focus on the academic paper-to-poster generation task and introduce PosterBench, comprising a 100-paper Main Track spanning five disciplines and PosterBench-mini, a shared 10-paper subset for controlled evaluation. On the PosterBench Main Track, AutoDesign achieves the highest score of 78.32, surpassing the closed-source commercial system Claude Design by 7.45 points. Across seven controlled code-agent-model configurations, integrating the learned DesignHarness consistently improves performance, increasing the average PosterBench Score from 54.99 to 67.39 (+12.4%). In a fully autonomous long-horizon loop, it executes 253 tool calls and 11 editing turns within 40 minutes for under $3, reaching average conference-poster quality in human evaluation. A system-blind human study further demonstrates that AutoDesign achieves the highest human preference among evaluated systems.

</details>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [92] [RoboSynChallenge: Mastering Real-World Dexterity via Generalizing Synthesized Manipulation Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12416)
*Runyi Zhao,Ruixin Wu,Chengkun Li,Hongrui Zhang,Ang Li,Ruixing Jin,Yueci Deng,Yingying Guo,Lihe Ding,Shaocong Dong,Tianfan Xue,Yanjun Gao,Yudong Luo,Pascal Poupart,Simo Wu,Kui Jia,Wei-shi Zheng,Guiliang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboSynChallenge是一项竞赛，提出了一个统一基准，通过大规模合成数据生成与标准化的真实世界机器人评估相结合，来评测和推进机器人操作策略的泛化能力。比赛提供多种基线实现（Transformer、Diffusion、VLA、World-Action-Model），最终仅在未见过的真实操作环境中进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能中机器人操作泛化仍是一大挑战，尽管模型架构和学习算法进展迅速，但真实世界数据的稀缺和多样性不足严重限制了进展。现有研究缺乏统一的标准来评测操控策略在跨任务、环境和难度上的泛化性能。

Method: 该竞赛框架将大规模合成数据生成与标准化的真实世界机器人评估相结合。参赛者被鼓励利用合成的状态-动作轨迹来改进通用策略学习，并提供包括Transformer、Diffusion、Vision-Language-Action以及World-Action-Model等多类基线实现以确保可复现性和可比性。最终评估仅在未见的真实操作环境中进行。

Result: 论文主要介绍了RoboSynChallenge竞赛的设计框架和评估协议。通过将可扩展的仿真训练与严格的真实世界验证相结合，旨在推动开发广泛能力、数据高效且自适应的操作系统的进展。

Conclusion: RoboSynChallenge通过提供统一基准和合成-真实数据协同的训练评测范式，有望加速面向真正通用机器人智能的操控策略发展，弥合仿真与真实之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Achieving generalizable robotic manipulation remains a central challenge in embodied intelligence. Despite rapid advances in model architectures and learning algorithms, progress is often limited by the scarcity and narrow diversity of real-world data. The RoboSynChallenge competition introduces a unified benchmark to evaluate and advance the generalizability of manipulation policies across a spectrum of tasks, environments, and difficulty levels. To alleviate the shortage of realistic data, the challenge integrates large-scale synthetic data generation with standardized real-world robotic evaluation. Participants are encouraged to leverage synthesized state-action trials to improve general-purpose policy learning, while final assessments are conducted exclusively on unseen real-world manipulation environments. Baseline implementations, including Transformer-, Diffusion-, Vision-Language-Action, and World-Action-Model-based policies, are provided to ensure reproducibility and comparability. By coupling scalable simulation-based training with rigorous real-world validation, RoboSynChallenge aims to foster the development of broadly capable, data-efficient, and adaptable manipulation systems, thereby paving the way toward truly general robotic intelligence.

</details>


### [93] [Attune: A Self-Annotation Tool for Understanding Robot Operator Attention Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12650)
*Puqi Zhou,Sungsoo Ray Hong,David Porfirio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了Attune——一种预部署的引导工具，通过将操作员的眼动视为机器人行为设计线索，帮助多机器人监督界面的设计者理解如何通过改变机器人行为来捕获、维持或释放操作员注意力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人监督中，操作员需同时监督多台机器人，管理操作员注意力是关键挑战。目前缺乏关于如何通过改变机器人行为来捕获、维持或释放操作员注意力的实证指导，这限制了界面设计的有效性。

Method: 作者创建了名为Attune的预部署引导工具，该工具自动识别有意义的注视转移时刻，提供AI辅助标注注视转移原因，并输出操作员注视模式的摘要供其审查。通过用户研究，参与者标注了吸引其注意力的视觉触发因素来评估该工具。

Result: 研究揭示了观察到的注视模式中存在差异，并展示了Attune如何帮助表征操作员注意力特征。

Conclusion: Attune通过自动化眼动分析和注释辅助，为多机器人监督界面设计提供了实证基础，使设计者能够根据不同的操作员注意力特征校准机器人行为，弥合了机器人行为设计与操作员注意力管理之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Deploying robot fleets in complex, real-world environments requires human operators to supervise multiple robots simultaneously. Managing operator attention is a fundamental challenge of designing multi-robot supervision interfaces, encompassing both feed layout and feed content (i.e., robot behavior design). Thus far, designers lack empirical guidance on the latter-how to change a robot's behavior to capture, sustain, or relinquish operator attention during multi-robot supervision. In our vision of the future, designers should be able to use this guidance to calibrate robot behavior to different operator attention profiles. Treating operator eye gaze as a robot behavior design clue, we created a pre-deployment elicitation tool called Attune. Attune automatically identifies when meaningful gaze shifts occur, provides AI assistance for annotating why shifts occurred, and outputs a summary of operator gaze patterns for operator review. We evaluated Attune through a user study in which participants annotated the visual triggers that drew their attention. Our findings unveil variation in observed gaze patterns and reveal how Attune helps characterize operator attention.

</details>


### [94] [FUSE: Active Functional Affordance Grounding through Adaptive Semantic-Geometric Evidence Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12683)
*Zhou Chen,Sathyanarayanan N. Aakur*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出主动功能可供性接地（Active Functional Affordance Grounding）这一新任务，并引入FUSE框架，通过自适应的语义-几何证据采集机制，结合显式不确定性驱动的探索与学习式摊销规划器，使智能体能够主动探索场景并定位满足功能查询的对象，在Habitat基准上取得了最高的非oracle接地性能，同时计算开销比全显式探索降低1.33倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体常需基于对象的功能而非身份来识别和交互，但现有可供性接地方法仅从固定视角操作，缺乏在功能线索被遮挡或不完整时决定"看哪里"的机制。

Method: 提出FUSE（自适应语义-几何证据采集框架），将显式不确定性驱动的探索与学习式摊销规划器相结合，高效选择信息量最大的视角；并构建基于Habitat的基准用于评估主动功能接地任务。

Result: FUSE在非oracle接地性能上达到最高水平，计算量比全显式探索降低1.33倍，且在多种可供性知识来源下均保持有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将显式探索与学习式规划结合，FUSE能够高效且有效地完成主动功能可供性接地任务，为具身智能体在复杂场景中基于功能进行对象定位提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Embodied agents must often identify and interact with objects based on their function rather than their identity, requiring them to actively acquire observations that reveal discriminative functional evidence. Existing affordance grounding methods operate from fixed viewpoints and lack mechanisms for deciding where to look when functional cues are occluded or incomplete. We introduce Active Functional Affordance Grounding, a new task in which an agent sequentially explores a scene to identify and spatially ground an object satisfying a functional query. To address this problem, we propose FUSE, an adaptive semantic-geometric evidence acquisition framework that combines explicit uncertainty-driven exploration with a learned amortized planner to efficiently select informative viewpoints. We further introduce a Habitat-based benchmark for evaluating active functional grounding. Experiments show that FUSE achieves the highest observed non-oracle grounding performance while reducing computation by 1.33x relative to fully explicit exploration, and remains effective across multiple affordance knowledge sources.

</details>


### [95] [SAP-Nav: Spatial Semantic Representation Meets Active Perception for Hierarchical Open-Vocabulary Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12707)
*Xuetong Pei,Jian Liu,Vidura Munasinghe,Bo Miao,U-Xuan Tan,Wenrui Ding,Na Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SAP-Nav 是一个完全在线、零样本的分层开放词汇目标导航框架，通过主动感知构建可查询的空间语义表示，并采用主动视点验证来确保目标识别的高置信度，在 LangMap 和 HM3D-OVON 基准上取得最佳性能，其中区域级导航的 SR 相比基于训练的方法提升 12.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层次开放词汇目标导航（OVON）要求智能体在未见环境中遵循自由形式指令，通过场景、房间、区域和实例级别线索定位目标。现有方法（如 LangMap）已形式化该设置，但在部分观测条件下难以可靠解决：空间定位需要持续的环境级证据，而目标验证需要清晰且具有区分度的候选视图。

Method: SAP-Nav 提出两个核心组件：(1) 从主动获取的房间视图增量构建可查询的空间语义表示，使智能体能在任何已探索位置执行空间语义查询；(2) 主动视点验证机制，评估当前观测是否提供足够证据，若不足则重新定位到信息量更丰富的视点，再基于类别和属性约束验证候选目标。该方法无需任务特定训练或预计算场景地图，兼容分层和标准类别级 OVON。

Result: 在 LangMap 和 HM3D-OVON 基准上，SAP-Nav 取得整体最佳性能，其中区域级导航的成功率（SR）相比基于训练的方法提升 12.2%。真实机器人实验进一步验证了该方法的实际可行性。

Conclusion: SAP-Nav 通过主动感知策略有效解决了层次 OVON 中空间定位与目标验证之间的信息需求矛盾，在多个基准上取得最优结果，且零样本、全在线特性使其具备较强的实际部署潜力。代码将在论文被接收后公开。

Abstract: Hierarchical open-vocabulary object navigation (OVON) requires agents to follow free-form instructions that may specify targets through scene-, room-, region-, and instance-level cues in unseen environments. Although recent work LangMap has formalized this setting, reliably solving it under partial observations remains challenging: spatial grounding requires persistent environment-level evidence, whereas target verification requires clear and discriminative candidate views. We present SAP-Nav, a fully online, zero-shot framework that addresses both requirements through active perception. SAP-Nav incrementally constructs a Queryable Spatial-Semantic Representation from actively acquired room views, enabling spatial semantic queries from any explored location. It further employs Active Viewpoint Verification to assess whether the current observation provides sufficient evidence and, when necessary, reposition the agent to a more informative viewpoint before verifying candidates against category and attribute constraints. Although designed for hierarchical OVON, SAP-Nav supports both hierarchical and standard category-level OVON without task-specific training or precomputed scene maps. Experiments on LangMap and HM3D-OVON show that SAP-Nav achieves the overall best performance, including a 12.2% improvement in SR over training-based methods on region-level navigation. Real-world robot experiments further demonstrate its practical feasibility. Code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [96] [Genetic Fuzzy System-Based Multi-Robot Coordination for Planetary Missions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12755)
*Daegyun Choi,Donghoon Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于遗传模糊系统的多机器人系统（MRS）分散式协作搬运方法，在非结构化环境中最小化总路径长度并避开障碍物。通过高程图进行地形可通行性分析，将地图转化为二维可通行性地图；利用遗传算法在多种场景下优化模糊推理系统（FIS）以生成机器人速度，最终在测试环境中验证其性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人协作物体搬运任务在非结构化环境中面临路径长度最小化和障碍规避的挑战，需要一种能够在复杂地形下高效协同的分散式控制方法。

Method: 首先基于高程图进行坡度相关的可通行性分析，将非可通行区域识别为障碍并转化为二维可通行性地图。随后设计模糊推理系统（FIS）生成机器人在搬运物体至目标位置时的速度，并通过遗传算法在局部极小值、目标靠近障碍物、杂乱环境等多种场景下优化FIS参数。训练后的FIS模型在转换后的可通行性地图测试环境中通过多个场景进行验证。

Result: 训练后的FIS模型成功应用于测试环境（转换后的可通行性地图），并通过多个场景进行了验证，实现了多机器人在非结构化环境下的协作物体搬运任务。

Conclusion: 所提出的分散式遗传模糊系统方法能够有效解决多机器人在非结构化环境中的协作搬运问题，在规避障碍物的同时最小化系统总路径长度，且通过遗传算法优化的模糊推理系统具备良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: This paper proposes a decentralized approach for a multi-robot system (MRS) using a genetic fuzzy system to perform a collaborative object transportation task that minimizes the total path length of the MRS in unstructured environment while avoiding obstacles. For an environment given by an elevation map, terrain traversability analysis with respect to the slope is performed to reduce the dimension and identify non-traversable areas that can be considered as obstacles, and the given map is converted into a traversability map in two dimensional space. In the training process, proposed fuzzy inference systems (FISs) to generate the MRS's velocity for transporting an object to a target position are optimized by a genetic algorithm with several scenarios, such as a local minima, a target that is close to an obstacle, and a cluttered environment. The trained FIS models are applied to the testing environment, which is the converted traversability map, and validated using multiple scenarios.

</details>


### [97] [AirForesight: Current-to-Future Spatial Map Imagination with Cross-Space Planning Consistency for UAV-VLN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12835)
*Yutong Liu,Xiaojie Li,Mingzhu Xu,Jianlong Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AirForesight，一个面向无人机视觉-语言导航的"当前到未来"空间地图想象框架。通过当前地图重建与未来轨迹预测的联合监督，在结构化因果注意力下将当前空间知识传播到未来地图推理，并引入跨空间规划一致性损失，在OpenUAV和AerialVLN-S上取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机视觉-语言导航方法大多将视觉-语言输入直接映射为动作，缺乏显式的场景grounding和未来感知的空间推理能力，在复杂户外环境中难以做出合理的三维运动决策。

Method: AirForesight框架包含三个核心组件：(1)从多视角观测学习结构化当前地图表示，并通过当前地图重建与未来轨迹预测联合监督；(2)在结构化因果注意力下将当前空间知识传播到未来地图推理；(3)将当前与未来表示聚合以预测下一个3D航点，并引入跨空间规划一致性损失使地图空间轨迹方向与专家动作方向保持一致。

Result: 在OpenUAV和AerialVLN-S两个基准上的实验以及大量消融研究表明，该框架取得了强性能，验证了方法的有效性与稳定性。

Conclusion: AirForesight通过显式构建当前-未来的空间地图想象机制，为UAV-VLN提供了更可靠的场景grounding与未来感知推理，显著提升了导航表现。跨空间规划一致性损失进一步强化了空间想象与真实导航决策之间的对齐。

Abstract: Unmanned Aerial Vehicle Vision-Language Navigation (UAV-VLN) requires agents to follow language instructions, infer spatial structure from sparse multi-view observations, and execute feasible 3D motion in complex outdoor environments. Despite recent progress with large language models, most existing methods still map vision-language inputs directly to actions, providing limited explicit scene grounding and future-aware spatial reasoning. We propose AirForesight, a current-to-future spatial map imagination framework for UAV-VLN. AirForesight first learns a structured current-map representation from multi-view observations. This representation is jointly supervised by current-map reconstruction and future-trajectory prediction, encouraging it to encode both present scene structure and future motion intent. Under structured causal attention, the current spatial knowledge is propagated to future-map reasoning, and the resulting current and future representations are aggregated to predict the next 3D waypoint. To make spatial imagination more relevant to navigation, we introduce a cross-space planning consistency loss that encourages directional agreement between the predicted map-space trajectory and the expert action direction derived from the ground-truth waypoint displacement. Experiments on OpenUAV and AerialVLN-S, together with extensive ablations, demonstrate strong performance and support the effectiveness and stability of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [98] [ASPIRE-VINS: Adaptive Spline-based Visual-inertial Navigation System With Robust 3D Measurement Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12840)
*Kwangyik Jung,Eungchang Mason Lee,Taekjun Oh,Hyun Myung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ASPIRE-VINS，一个结合自适应节点放置（AKP）、多分辨率样条（MRS）和3D测量空间残差（3D-MSR）的连续时间视觉惯性导航系统，在多样运动与感知条件下实现了与基线相当或更优的轨迹精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散时间VINS方法（如IMU预积分）精度高、效率好，但基于关键帧的表示在需要对任意时间戳评估残差或需要运动相关的时间分辨率时不够灵活。连续时间样条虽然可以平滑表示轨迹，但均匀分布的节点在快速动态区间欠表示、在静态区间过度参数化，缺乏自适应能力。

Method: ASPIRE-VINS主要由三个技术组件构成：(1) 自适应节点放置（AKP），根据局部运动变化分配样条节点；(2) 多分辨率样条（MRS），在切空间中添加有界局部细化；(3) 3D测量空间残差（3D-MSR），通过对齐变换后的特征与3D测量空间中的校准观测射线来实现方位一致性约束。

Result: 实验结果表明，ASPIRE-VINS在多种运动与感知条件下相比现有基线方法取得了具有竞争力或更低的轨迹误差，验证了运动自适应连续时间轨迹建模的有效性。

Conclusion: 自适应节点分配与多分辨率细化相结合，能够在连续时间VINS中有效应对多样化的运动动态，在保持连续时间表示灵活性的同时提升轨迹估计精度。

Abstract: Visual-inertial navigation systems estimate six-degree-of-freedom motion by fusing visual and inertial data. Modern discrete-time methods with IMU preintegration provide strong accuracy and efficiency, but keyframe-based representations can be less flexible when residuals must be evaluated at arbitrary timestamps or when motion-dependent temporal resolution is needed. Continuous-time splines address this issue by representing the trajectory as a smooth temporal function, but uniformly spaced knots can under-represent rapid dynamics or over-parameterize static intervals. This letter proposes ASPIRE-VINS, a continuous-time VINS framework that combines adaptive knot placement (AKP), multi-resolution splines (MRS), and 3D measurement-space residuals (3D-MSR). AKP allocates knots according to local motion variation, MRS adds bounded local refinement in tangent space, and 3D-MSR provides bearing consistency by aligning transformed features with calibrated observation rays in 3D measurement space. Experiments show that ASPIRE-VINS achieves competitive or lower trajectory errors than the compared baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness of motion-adaptive continuous-time trajectory modeling under diverse motion and sensing conditions.

</details>


### [99] [BrainWAM: Action-Space Coordination of Semantic Priors and Predictive Dynamics for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12854)
*Bing Zhan,Shuyao Shang,Jiahao Gu,Shuo Lu,Yuan Xu,Zhao Wang,Yida Wang,Xueyang Zhang,Kun Zhan,Lue Fan,Zhaoxiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BrainWAM是一个结构化动作空间协调框架，将语义推理（VLA）与预测性世界建模（WAM）转化为两个专门的面向动作的路径，并在紧凑动作表征层面进行对齐，解决了朴素组合中注意力分配不匹配的问题。在NAVSIM v1和v2上达到SOTA性能（89.5 PDMS和89.6 EPDMS）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶需要在语义约束和预测动力学下同时进行规划，但现有端到端方法（VLA和WAM）通常只强调其中一方面。简单的联合token级注意力组合存在注意力分配不匹配问题——语义捷径主导共享注意力空间并抑制预测动力学，因此需要一个统一的规划器同时利用两种先验知识。

Method: 受神经科学中复杂行为源于功能特化系统间协调的启发，BrainWAM将语义推理和预测世界建模转换为两个专门化的面向动作路径，并在紧凑动作表征层面进行对齐。此外，引入异步修正流（asynchronous rectified-flow）推断策略，通过解耦视频和动作去噪来缩短推断延迟同时保留规划相关的预测上下文。

Result: BrainWAM在NAVSIM v1上达到89.5 PDMS，在NAVSIM v2上达到89.6 EPDMS，均取得SOTA性能，一致优于仅用VLA或仅用WAM的方法。

Conclusion: BrainWAM证明了将语义先验与预测动力学在动作空间层面结构化协调是自动驾驶规划的有效且实用的方向，为自动驾驶系统提供了有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous driving requires planning under both semantic constraints and predictive dynamics. Existing end-to-end driving approaches, however, typically emphasize only one side of this requirement: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models exploit VLM priors for semantic reasoning, while World Action Models (WAMs) provide future-aware prediction through generative world modeling. This naturally motivates a unified planner that can leverage both semantic priors and predictive dynamics. However, we find that a naive combination through joint token-level attention suffers from an attention-allocation mismatch, where semantic shortcuts dominate the shared attention space and suppress predictive dynamics. Inspired by neuroscience evidence that complex behavior arises from coordination among functionally specialized systems, we propose BrainWAM, a structured action-space coordination framework that converts semantic reasoning and predictive world modeling into two specialized action-oriented pathways, and aligns them at the level of compact action representations. We further introduce an asynchronous rectified-flow inference strategy with decoupled video and action denoising, which shortens inference latency while preserving planning-relevant predictive context. BrainWAM reaches state-of-the-art performance on both NAVSIM v1 (89.5 PDMS) and NAVSIM v2 (89.6 EPDMS), consistently outperforming VLA-only or WAM-only methods, highlighting BrainWAM as a practical and promising direction for autonomous driving systems.

</details>


### [100] [HumanoidVLN: A Physics-Grounded Simulator and Benchmark for Vision-Language Navigation Across Diverse Humanoid Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12860)
*Quan-Dung Pham,Anh Dao,The-Anh Nguyen,Minh Nguyen-Dinh,Phuong Nam Dang,Tri Pham,Hung Tran,Bach Dao,Tuyen P. Le,Truong Nguyen,Quan Nguyen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HumanoidVLN，一个基于NVIDIA Isaac Sim构建的物理仿真器与基准平台，用于人形机器人的视觉-语言导航（VLN）研究。平台支持四种不同构型的人形机器人（Unitree G1、H1、Internal-A、Internal-B），通过层级控制栈结合强化学习运动策略与PD/MPC路径跟踪器实现双足运动。基于多智能体指令生成管线构建了933个碰撞感知参考片段，并在四个VLN模型与四个机器人构型上完成评测。JanusVLN取得最佳成功率43.55%和nDTW 48.38。20个片段的sim-to-real验证显示导航误差与仿真强相关（r=0.935）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN基准未能解决人形机器人特有的挑战：双足运动引入轮式智能体不具备的物理约束、人形机器人构型在不同平台上差异显著、以及运动引发的相机动态导致的自我中心观测畸变。因此需要一个物理真实的人形机器人VLN仿真与评测平台。

Method: 基于NVIDIA Isaac Sim构建物理仿真器，采用层级控制栈（强化学习运动策略+可互换的PD或MPC路径跟踪器）驱动4种不同高度和DoF的人形机器人。场景来自艺术家设计场景与3D高斯溅射重建（筛选可导航面积>100m²）。指令由"生成器-评审器-改写器"多智能体管线配合人工验证生成，每个参考片段配备一条细粒度指令和三种风格变体。评测四个主流VLN模型并开展sim-to-real迁移实验。

Result: 四个模型与四种构型全面评测中，JanusVLN获得最高平均成功率43.55%和nDTW 48.38。20个片段的sim-to-real试验（DualVLN+Unitree G1）中，导航误差与仿真结果强相关（r=0.935），平均绝对误差0.68m，平均轨迹相似度0.782±0.188 nDTW。

Conclusion: 实验结果揭示了VLN模型、控制器与人形机器人构型在真实物理执行下的交互关系。该平台验证了仿真到现实的强迁移性，为人形机器人VLN研究提供了可扩展的基准基础设施。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) for humanoid robots poses challenges existing benchmarks fail to address: bipedal locomotion imposes physical constraints absent from wheeled agents, humanoid morphologies vary across platforms, and egocentric observations are distorted by locomotion-induced camera dynamics. We present HumanoidVLN, a physics-grounded simulator and benchmark for VLN across diverse humanoid embodiments. Built on NVIDIA Isaac Sim, our platform supports an extensible set of humanoid configurations, demonstrated on four robots (Unitree G1, Unitree H1, Internal-A, Internal-B) spanning 10-12 lower-body DoF and heights from 1.17m to 1.80m, via a hierarchical control stack combining a reinforcement learning locomotion policy with interchangeable PD or MPC path trackers. New robots and VLN models integrate with minimal effort; we demonstrate compatibility with NaVILA, DualVLN, StreamVLN, and JanusVLN. Environments are drawn from artist-designed scenes and 3D Gaussian Splatting reconstructions, filtered for navigable areas exceeding 100 square meters. Instructions are generated by a dual generator-reviewer plus paraphraser multi-agent pipeline with human-in-the-loop verification, yielding 933 collision-aware reference episodes, each paired with one fine-grained instruction and three coarse-grained stylistic variants (formal, natural, casual). Across four models and four embodiments, JanusVLN achieves the highest mean success rate of 43.55% and nDTW of 48.38. In a 20-episode sim-to-real pilot with DualVLN and the Unitree G1, navigation errors correlate strongly (r=0.935), with a mean absolute difference of 0.68m and mean trajectory similarity of 0.782 (+/-0.188) nDTW. These results highlight the interaction between VLN models, controllers, and humanoid embodiments under physical execution. Code, benchmark, and data will be released upon acceptance at https://humanoid-vln.github.io/.

</details>


### [101] [AMR-Pose: An Active LED Marker-Based Relative Pose Estimation Framework With Probabilistic Switching PnP for Cooperative AUVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12866)
*Zeyu Sha,Xiaorui Wang,Mingyang Yang,Feitian Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AMR-Pose框架，利用主动LED标记（一个红色中心LED加三个蓝色周边LED）和概率切换PnP估计器（PSwPnP），实现自主水下机器人（AUV）之间鲁棒的六自由度相对位姿估计，应对浑浊、光照变化等多重水下光学退化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AUV之间的可靠相对位姿估计对协同海洋探索、采样和多机器人协调至关重要。然而水下环境的光学退化（浊度、光照变化、反射、特征间歇性遮挡）严重阻碍了基于视觉的相对定位，现有方法难以保证鲁棒性。

Method: 本文提出AMR-Pose框架：(1)设计紧凑式标记模块，包含一个红色中心LED和三个蓝色周边LED，安装于领航AUV上，在水下复杂条件下提供独特视觉特征；(2)开发概率切换Perspective-n-Point估计器（PSwPnP），融合SE(3)上的李群位姿传播、概率标记关联和可见性自适应测量融合，实现鲁棒六自由度相对位姿估计；(3)根据标记可见性动态调整估计过程，在部分观测和可见性切换期间保持几何一致性和时间稳定性。

Result: 在水箱实验中借助运动捕捉系统提供真值，验证了AMR-Pose在挑战性水下条件下实现准确、平滑且鲁棒的相对位姿估计。闭环领导者-跟随者实验进一步证明了该方法在协作水下机器人实时相对位姿反馈中的可行性。

Conclusion: AMR-Pose通过主动LED标记与概率切换PnP估计器的结合，为AUV协同任务提供了鲁棒的相对位姿估计方案，在光学退化环境下保持高精度与时间稳定性，并已验证可支持实时闭环控制，具有实际部署价值。

Abstract: Reliable relative pose estimation between autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) is critical for cooperative ocean exploration, sampling, and multi-robot coordination. However, achieving robust vision-based relative localization in underwater environments remains challenging due to severe optical degradation, including turbidity, illumination variations, reflections, and intermittent feature occlusions. This paper presents AMR-Pose, an active LED marker-based relative pose estimation framework for cooperative AUVs. A compact marker module consisting of one red central LED and three blue peripheral LEDs is developed and integrated onto the leader AUV to provide distinctive visual features under complex underwater conditions. Building upon the detected marker observations, a probabilistic switching Perspective-n-Point estimator (PSwPnP) is developed by combining Lie-group pose propagation on $SE(3)$, probabilistic marker association, and visibility-adaptive measurement fusion for robust six-degree-of-freedom relative pose estimation. The proposed framework dynamically adapts the estimation process according to marker visibility, maintaining geometric consistency and temporal stability during partial observations and visibility transitions. Extensive water-tank experiments with motion-capture ground truth validate that AMR-Pose achieves accurate, smooth, and robust relative pose estimation under challenging underwater conditions. Closed-loop leader-follower experiments further demonstrate its feasibility for real-time relative pose feedback in cooperative underwater robotics.

</details>


### [102] [Temporal GRPO: Beyond Trajectory-Level Credit in Vision-Language-Action Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13026)
*Yao Zhou,Hang Gao,Fengge Wu,Changwen Zheng,Wenwen Qiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Temporal GRPO，通过在VLA后训练中构建可检测的任务阶段，将优势值按轨迹的阶段间隔分配，解决传统GRPO中轨迹级信用混淆问题，提升任务成功率与样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的GRPO-based VLA后训练将一个rollout级别的优势值应用于轨迹中的每个动作。当一条轨迹前期完成了多个有效阶段但后续失败时，早期的正确动作会因后期失败而被错误惩罚，即"轨迹级信用混淆"（trajectory-level credit aliasing）问题，这限制了基于稀疏奖励的强化学习在机器人操作任务中的效果。

Method: Temporal GRPO通过三个步骤解决信用混淆问题：1) 构建可检测的任务阶段；2) 将每条rollout与阶段特定的动作区间对齐；3) 仅对进入相同阶段的rollouts进行比较以生成阶段优势值。这些阶段优势值在单次策略更新中被应用于对应的动作区间。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0数据集上，Temporal GRPO提升了任务成功率和样本效率，并在不同任务长度上保持一致增益。在LIBERO-Long上的受控更新实验表明，该方法能保留共享的前置阶段，并将改进集中在rollout结果分叉的第一阶段。

Conclusion: 通过时间维度的阶段级信用分配，Temporal GRPO有效缓解了轨迹级信用混淆问题，为VLA策略的稀疏奖励后训练提供了更精确、高效的优化信号。

Abstract: Outcome-driven reinforcement learning offers a scalable way to post-train vision-language-action (VLA) policies from sparse task-success feedback. In common GRPO-based VLA post-training, one rollout-level advantage is applied to every action in the trajectory. A rollout that completes several valid stages but fails later can therefore penalize the actions that produced its earlier progress. We call this trajectory-level credit aliasing. Temporal GRPO addresses this problem by constructing detectable task stages, aligning each rollout with stage-specific action intervals, and comparing only rollouts that have entered the same stage. The resulting stage advantages are applied to their corresponding intervals in a single policy update. On RoboTwin 2.0, Temporal GRPO improves task success and sample efficiency, with consistent gains across task horizons. Controlled updates on LIBERO-Long preserve shared prerequisite stages and concentrate improvement at the first stage where rollout outcomes diverge.

</details>


### [103] [H2R-Bench: Benchmarking Human-to-Robot Manipulation Video Generation in World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13049)
*Dingyi Rong,Yue Shi,Chaofan Ma,Jiezhang Cao,Zongrui Wang,Zeyu Zhang,Yao Mu,Guangtao Zhai,Ning Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了H2R-Bench基准，用于评估跨具身（人类到机器人）操作视频生成任务。该基准将人类第一人称操作视频转化为指定机器人具身下的操作视频，并通过五个维度（目标状态完成、动作事件完成、功能接触迁移、具身正确性和通用视频质量）对十一个视频生成模型进行系统评测，揭示了现有视频世界模型在人类到机器人操作迁移方面仍存在明显局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模操作数据对机器人学习至关重要，但机器人演示数据采集成本高、难以规模化。丰富的第一人称人类操作视频虽提供了大量行为经验，但人类手部与机器人末端执行器之间的差异使得跨具身迁移极具挑战。尽管视频世界模型有望从人类观察中合成机器人视角的操作视频，但其跨具身迁移能力尚未被系统探索，因此需要一个专门的基准来评估这一能力。

Method: H2R-Bench构建了包含人类演示视频、目标具身约束和源标注（任务目标、动作事件、功能接触、物体响应）的基准实例。评估通过五个维度展开：目标状态完成、动作事件完成、功能接触迁移、具身正确性和通用视频质量。对六个操作家族、两个机器人具身的十一个视频生成模型进行了系统评测。

Result: 评测显示，当前视频世界模型在人类到机器人操作迁移方面能力有限：即使最先进的模型在具身一致性、功能交互和任务执行方面也常常失败。这说明现有模型尚不能有效弥合人类与机器人之间的具身差距。

Conclusion: H2R-Bench为评估视频世界模型是否能够弥合人类到机器人的具身鸿沟、将人类操作观察转化为机器人训练资源提供了一个系统化的诊断框架，同时指出了当前技术在这一任务上的不足与改进方向。

Abstract: Large-scale manipulation data is essential for robot learning, yet collecting robot demonstrations remains expensive and difficult to scale. Meanwhile, abundant egocentric human manipulation videos provide rich behavioral experiences, but transferring them across embodiments remains challenging due to differences between human hands and robotic end-effectors. Recent advances in video world models offer a promising pathway to synthesize robot-centric manipulation videos from human observations, while their cross-embodiment transfer capability remains largely unexplored. Therefore, we introduce H2R-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating cross-embodiment human-to-robot manipulation video generation, where models transform egocentric human demonstrations into robot manipulation videos under specified embodiments. Each benchmark instance contains a human demonstration video, target embodiment constraints, and source-grounded annotations covering task goals, action events, functional contacts, and object responses. H2R-Bench evaluates generated videos through five dimensions, including goal-state completion, action-event completion, functional contact transfer, embodiment correctness, and general video quality. We benchmark eleven state-of-the-art video generation models across six manipulation families and two robot embodiments. Our evaluation reveals that current video world models remain limited in human-to-robot manipulation transfer: even leading models often fail in embodiment consistency, functional interaction, and task execution. H2R-Bench provides a systematic diagnostic framework for evaluating whether video world models can bridge the human-to-robot embodiment gap and convert human manipulation observations into robot-centric training resources.

</details>


### [104] [Semantic Radiance Fields as Simulators for Spatial Reasoning in Real-World Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13095)
*Nico Heider,Michał Jan Włodarczyk,Katarzyna Wasielewska-Michniewska,Przemysław Hołda,Martin Schieck,Marcin Paprzycki,Maria Ganzha,Bogdan Franczyk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出使用语义辐射场（SRF）作为具身智能体空间推理的训练与评估模拟器，通过将预训练视觉模型的2D语义分割结果提升到3D辐射场中，统一编码几何、外观和逐类语义身份，在保持真实感的同时提供语义真值，并展示了在果园摘苹果任务中的示例应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的空间推理环境在几何保真度和语义可查询性之间存在矛盾：合成模拟器提供语义真值但缺乏真实感，基于真实世界重建的模拟器外观真实但缺少默认的语义真值标注。需要一种统一的表示来兼顾两者。

Method: 提出语义辐射场（SRF）作为空间推理智能体的模拟器。SRF将预训练视觉模型产生的2D语义分割从真实场景的带位姿RGB图像中提升为3D辐射场，联合编码几何、外观和逐类语义身份。该表示支持新视图合成、语义查询和自由空间查询，并与物理引擎集成。

Result: SRF能够在统一的接地表示中实现新视图合成、语义查询和自由空间查询，从而高效生成多样的真实世界环境来训练和评估空间推理模型。示例应用展示了在果园摘苹果任务中辐射场为物理引擎提供相机渲染、语义真值和不占用空间查询的能力。

Conclusion: 语义辐射场作为模拟器能够统一真实感外观与精确语义标注，为空间推理智能体的训练和评估提供了一种兼具保真度和可查询性的方案，具有实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Training and evaluating spatial reasoning in embodied agents requires diverse environments that are both geometrically faithful and semantically queryable. Synthetic simulators offer ground truth semantics but sacrifice realism; simulators based on reconstructions of real-world environments have realistic appearance but lack ground truth semantics by default. We propose using Semantic Radiance Fields (SRF) as simulators for spatial reasoning agents. SRFs are a representation that unifies these requirements by lifting 2D semantic segmentations from pretrained vision models into a 3D radiance field that jointly encodes geometry, appearance, and per-class semantic identity. The resulting fields are reconstructed from posed RGB captures of real scenes and support novel-view synthesis, semantic and free-space queries within a single grounded representation. This enables the efficient generation of diverse real-world environments to train and evaluate spatial reasoning models. As an example application, we outline an SRF-driven simulator for an orchard apple-reaching task, in which the radiance field supplies camera rendering, semantic ground truth, and occupancy queries to a physics engine.

</details>


### [105] [S2-HWM: Sparse Event-Structured Hierarchical World Model for Long-Horizon Surgical Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13103)
*Shuzhe Zhang,Xin Zhu,Yinling Qian,Qiong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出S2-HWM，一种稀疏事件结构化层次世界模型，通过从原始潜轨迹中学习稀疏事件证据来协调事件级管理器和原始步工人，解决长时程手术机器人操作中奖励稀疏、交互变化间隔不规则的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程手术机器人操作任务奖励稀疏，有意义的交互变化发生间隔不规则。现有世界模型以原始步分辨率想象，无法显式建模可变时长的任务进度；人工指定阶段虽然能提供中间结构，但其任务特定边界难以与状态依赖的交互转换对齐。

Method: S2-HWM包含三个核心组件：(1) 从原始潜轨迹中学习稀疏事件证据；(2) 事件证据调度事件级管理器（manager）的目标更新，每个选定的潜目标条件化原始步工人（worker）的动作直至下一次更新；(3) 学习的事件证据形成可变时长片段供事件转换模型（ETM）使用，ETM预测边界随机状态、片段时长和累积片段奖励，通过链式预测提供超出原始想象视界的可变时长延续用于管理器学习，工人保留原始步actor-critic学习。

Result: 在基于SurRoL的PegTransfer任务上，S2-HWM实现了98.7%的成功率，比扁平GAS DreamerV3基线高出22.7个百分点。

Conclusion: S2-HWM通过事件结构化层次世界模型，将事件级预测与原始步学习相结合，有效解决了稀疏奖励长时程操作任务中的时序结构化问题，显著提升任务成功率。

Abstract: Long-horizon surgical robot manipulation is challenging because task rewards are sparse, while meaningful interaction changes occur at irregular intervals. Existing world-model agents typically imagine at primitive-step resolution, leaving variable-duration task progress implicit. Manually specified stages can provide intermediate structure, but their task specific boundaries are difficult to align with state-dependent interaction transitions. We propose S2-HWM, a Sparse Event-Structured Hierarchical World Model that learns sparse event evidence from primitive latent trajectories to coordinate an event-level manager and a primitive-step worker. The event evidence schedules manager goal updates, and each selected latent goal conditions the worker's primitive actions until the next update. The learned event evidence also forms variable-duration segments for an Event Transition Model (ETM), which predicts the next?boundary stochastic state, segment duration, and accumulated segment reward. Chaining these event-level predictions provides a variable-duration continuation beyond the primitive imagination horizon for manager learning, while the worker retains primitive-step actor-critic learning. On a SurRoL-based PegTransfer task, S2-HWM achieves a success rate of 98.7%, outperforming the flat GAS DreamerV3 baseline by 22.7 percentage points.

</details>


### [106] [FAM-DQ: A Dual-Quadrotor-Based Fully Actuated Aerial Manipulator for High-Torque Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13220)
*Xuwei Yang,Ruoyu Ren,Ziqian Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FAM-DQ，一种基于双四旋翼的完全驱动空中机械臂，通过被动关节连接双四旋翼推进模块并使用齿轮驱动伺服控制指向方向，实现解耦的6自由度末端执行器控制和高扭矩物理交互，最大扭矩1.019 N·m，总重0.447 kg，扭矩质量比达2.28 N·m/kg。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统欠驱动空中机械臂存在强位置-姿态耦合问题，而完全驱动平台设计通常面临结构复杂、载荷能力有限和扭矩输出不足的挑战，需要一种既能产生大交互力/力矩又保持末端执行器精确控制的方案。

Method: 通过在中央框架两端安装两个四旋翼推进模块（通过被动关节连接），并利用齿轮驱动伺服调节指向方向，实现6自由度解耦的末端执行器控制，兼具全方位操作能力和增强的扭矩输出。

Result: 轨迹跟踪、姿态跟踪、静态扭矩测量和螺丝拧紧等实验验证了设计有效性。FAM-DQ最大扭矩达1.019 N·m，总质量0.447 kg，扭矩质量比2.28 N·m/kg。

Conclusion: FAM-DQ成功解决了欠驱动空中机械臂的位置-姿态耦合与完全驱动平台的复杂度问题，以轻量化设计实现高扭矩物理交互能力，适用于要求精确末端控制和高交互力的空中操作任务。

Abstract: Aerial physical interaction requires aerial manipulation platforms to generate large interaction forces and torques while maintaining precise end-effector control. However, conventional underactuated aerial manipulators suffer from strong position-attitude coupling, whereas fully actuated platform designs often face structural complexity, limited payload capacity, and insufficient torque output. This paper presents FAM-DQ, a dual-quadrotor based fully actuated aerial manipulator designed for high-torque physical interaction tasks. By mounting two quadrotor propulsion modules at the ends of a central frame through passive joints, while using a gear-driven servo to regulate the pointing direction, FAM-DQ achieves decoupled $6$-DoF end-effector control with omnidirectional manipulation capability and enhanced torque output. Experiments including trajectory tracking, attitude tracking, static torque measurement, and screw driving validate the proposed design. FAM-DQ achieves a maximum torque of $1.019~\mathrm{N}\cdot\mathrm{m}$ with a total mass of $0.447~\mathrm{kg}$, corresponding to a torque-to-mass ratio of $2.28~\mathrm{N}\cdot\mathrm{m/kg}$.

</details>


### [107] [Manufacturing Complex Airtight Soft Pneumatic Actuators for Soft Robotics: Process Evaluation and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13233)
*Mohammed Abboodi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对复杂软体气动执行器的多种制造路线进行了面向制造的系统评估，包括热缩成型、硅胶浇铸、粉末/液体增材制造和FDM。通过工艺筛选、基线制造、失效分析和工艺改进，发现FDM最具适应性，其主导缺陷可通过工艺优化逐步消除。研究还表明气密性不仅取决于标称壁厚，还取决于挤出路径架构，最终提出了以制造约束与执行器架构兼容性为导向的设计-制造选择方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 制造复杂的软体气动执行器需要在几何保真度、柔顺性、结构完整性和气密性之间同时达到平衡，这一多目标要求使制造极具挑战性。现有研究缺乏对不同制造路线在复杂气动结构上的系统性对比与评估，无法区分固有工艺局限与可修正的制造缺陷，因此需要建立面向制造的设计方法以指导工艺选择。

Method: 研究采用四个阶段的评估框架：(1)工艺筛选，初选候选制造路线；(2)基线制造，按标准流程制备样品；(3)失效分析，识别各工艺的主导缺陷；(4)工艺改进，区分固有工艺限制与可纠正缺陷。评估对象涵盖热缩成型、硅胶浇铸、粉末基和液体基增材制造（包括数字光处理DLP）以及熔融沉积建模（FDM）。

Result: 热缩成型受限于几何贴合度；浇铸受限于模具可达性和粘合界面；粉末基方法受限于封闭通道内残留粉末；DLP受限于材料特性和后处理要求。FDM表现出最灵活的制造适应性，其主导缺陷可通过工艺优化逐步减小。此外发现气密性受挤出路径架构影响，且当内部后处理可达性受限时，免支撑几何十分关键。

Conclusion: 研究建立了面向制造的设计（design-for-manufacturing）实用方法，工艺选择应基于执行器架构与制造约束之间的兼容性。该思路为开发面向软体机器人应用的复杂、柔顺且气密的软体气动执行器提供了实际指导。

Abstract: Manufacturing complex soft pneumatic actuators remains challenging because geometric fidelity, compliance, structural integrity, and airtightness must be achieved simultaneously. This study presents a manufacturing-focused evaluation of several fabrication routes for complex pneumatic structures, including heat-shrink forming, silicone casting, powder- and liquid-based additive manufacturing, and fused deposition modeling (FDM). The processes were assessed through process screening, baseline fabrication, failure analysis, and process improvement to distinguish inherent process limitations from correctable manufacturing defects. Heat-shrink forming was limited by geometric conformity, casting by mold accessibility and bonded interfaces, powder-based methods by residual material trapped within enclosed passages, and digital light processing by the material properties and post-processing requirements of the investigated system. FDM provided the most adaptable route because its dominant defects could be progressively reduced through process optimization.
