The results further showed that airtightness depends not only on nominal wall thickness but also on extrusion-path architecture, while support-free geometry is important when access for internal post-processing is limited. These findings establish a practical design-for-manufacturing approach in which process selection is guided by the compatibility between actuator architecture and manufacturing constraints. The proposed approach provides practical guidance for developing complex, flexible, and airtight soft pneumatic actuators for soft robotic applications

</details>


### [108] [Predictive Relative-Velocity Steering for Safe Robotic Manipulator Teleoperation in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13284)
*Changhao Hu,Zeyi Liu,Songqiao Hu,Shuang Liu,Zihan Meng,Xiao He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个轻量级、模块化的主动碰撞避免框架，作用于末端执行器速度指令层面。通过基于时间到碰撞（TTC）与超调保护的预测模块预判碰撞，并利用Rodrigues旋转公式改变相对速度方向而不改变大小，从而避免人工势场法的死锁问题。模拟和物理实验均验证了其较高的碰撞避免成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥操作系统使机器人能执行类人灵巧运动，但在网络延迟或操作者注意力受限的情况下，人类操作者可能无法及时有效地避开突然出现的障碍物，造成安全风险。现有方法（如人工势场法）存在死锁等问题，因此需要一种轻量、模块化且高效的主动碰撞避免方案。

Method: 该方法为轻量级模块化框架，直接作用于末端执行器速度指令层。首先对点云进行预处理；然后基于时间到碰撞（TTC）并集成超调保护来预测潜在碰撞；随后使用Rodrigues旋转公式旋转相对速度向量。该偏转仅改变相对速度方向而保持其大小不变，从而缓解传统APF方法的死锁问题。预测模块补偿了复杂遥操作管道中引入的点云处理延迟，轻量级设计支持遥操作所需的高频控制。

Result: 在多种仿真场景中，该方法相比基线方法实现了更高的末端执行器碰撞避免率；在物理机器人系统上的实验进一步验证了其碰撞避免的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的主动碰撞避免框架在保持速度大小的前提下仅改变相对速度方向，有效解决了传统人工势场法的死锁问题，并通过预测模块补偿点云处理延迟，在仿真和物理实验中均展现良好的碰撞避免性能，适用于遥操作系统。

Abstract: Recent advances in teleoperation have enabled robotic manipulators to perform dexterous, human-arm-like motions. However, human operators may fail to avoid suddenly appearing obstacles promptly and effectively, particularly under network latency or limited attention, thereby creating safety risks. To address this issue, we propose a lightweight and modular framework for proactive collision avoidance, operating directly at the end-effector velocity-command level. After preprocessing the point cloud, the framework first predicts potential collisions based on time-to-collision (TTC) with integrated overshoot protection, and subsequently rotates the relative-velocity vector using Rodrigues' rotation formula. The deflection changes only the direction of the relative velocity while preserving its magnitude, thereby mitigating the deadlock problem commonly encountered by conventional artificial potential field (APF) methods. The prediction module compensates for point-cloud processing latency introduced by complex teleoperation pipelines, while the lightweight design enables the high-frequency control required for teleoperation. Simulations across diverse scenarios show that the proposed method achieves a higher end-effector collision avoidance rate than the baseline methods. Experiments on a physical robotic system further validate its collision-avoidance effectiveness.

</details>


### [109] [NestDex: Nested Policy Learning with Copilot Assisted Teleoperation for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13362)
*James Zhao,Jinhe Tang,Mingyuan Ba,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NestDex是一个嵌套式策略学习框架，通过学习的"手部技能"辅助收集灵巧操作演示，操作者仅需通过单自由度离合器控制手臂并调节手部技能，而非直接指定完整的手指轨迹。内部手部策略根据本体感受历史自适应运动，视觉-语言选择器为每个任务阶段激活合适的技能；生成的演示用于训练一个独立的外部视觉运动策略，在部署时同时控制手臂和手部，无需内部策略。实验表明NestDex提升了演示的可靠性和效率，并有效支持自主策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作能大幅提升机器人对物理世界的交互能力，但学习这些行为受限于收集一致、完整的任务演示的难度。与平行夹爪操作不同，灵巧任务要求操作者在整个任务过程中协调手臂运动与精确、接触密集的手指行为，这使演示收集变得极其困难。

Method: 提出NestDex嵌套策略学习框架：操作者控制手臂并通过单自由度离合器调节活动的手部技能，而非直接指定完整手指轨迹；内部手部策略从最新的本体感受历史中自适应调整运动，视觉-语言选择器为每个任务阶段激活合适技能；生成演示用于训练独立的外部视觉运动策略，部署时控制手臂与手部而无需内部策略；采用手部动作变分自编码器提供紧凑的手部动作目标，手臂指令保留在关节空间。

Result: 在真实世界的灵巧操作实验中，NestDex提高了演示的可靠性和效率，实证评估支持有效的自主策略学习。

Conclusion: NestDex通过将手部控制分解为可复用的学习技能，显著降低了灵巧操作演示收集的负担，同时训练出的外部策略在部署时无需内部策略即可直接控制手臂与手部，为灵巧操作的自主策略学习提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation promises substantially richer robot interaction with the physical world, but learning these behaviours remains constrained by the difficulty of collecting consistent, complete-task demonstrations. Unlike parallel-jaw manipulation, dexterous tasks require the operator to coordinate arm motion with precise, contact-rich finger behaviour throughout the task. We introduce NestDex, a nested policy-learning framework that reduces this burden by using learned hand skills to assist demonstration collection. The operator controls the arm and regulates the active hand skill through a single-DoF clutch, rather than directly specifying the full finger trajectory. The inner hand policy adapts its motion from the latest proprioceptive history, while a vision-language selector activates the appropriate skill for each task stage. The resulting demonstrations train a separate outer visuomotor policy that controls both the arm and hand without the inner policies at deployment. A hand-action variational autoencoder provides compact hand-action targets while retaining arm commands in joint space. Across real-world dexterous manipulation experiments, NestDex improves demonstration reliability and efficiency, and the resulting empirical evaluations support effective autonomous policy learning. Video Demo are available at project website https://aus.bot/research/nestdex.

</details>


### [110] [FIRE-VLA: Failure-Informed Self-Evolution for Vision-Language-Action Models in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13395)
*Hao Dou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出FIRE-VLA，一种失败感知的自进化框架，用于改进自动驾驶视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的强化学习。当GRPO中采样轨迹全部较差时，相对奖励信号无法识别失败区域之外的行为，FIRE-VLA通过将未解决的失败转化为特权监督来解决这一问题：低奖励、低多样性组触发从冻结的round-start模型副本进行自蒸馏，教师和学生参数规模相同，但仅教师能看到隐藏的未来轨迹。监督仅作用于答案token，同时GRPO保持活跃。在6,019个样本和150个保留nuScenes场景上，FIRE-VLA在保持相似单样本规划能力的同时，将G=4平均L2从1.848 m降至1.500 m，持续失败率从13.03%降至11.20%，且误差降低主要来自罕见严重rollout的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO在学习相对奖励差异时存在固有局限：当一组所有采样轨迹都很差时，相对信号只能对这些失败进行排序，无法识别失败区域之外的正确行为，导致策略难以突破失败模式。

Method: FIRE-VLA通过失败感知的自进化框架，将未解决的失败转化为特权监督。具体而言：1) 低奖励、低多样性组触发自蒸馏；2) 教师为冻结的round-start模型副本，与学生同参数规模但能观察隐藏的未来轨迹；3) 监督跟随学生生成前缀并仅限制在答案token；4) GRPO对每个组保持激活；5) 更新后的策略作为下一轮的教师，使失败分布随策略演化，无需更大的外部教师。

Result: 从相同的Qwen2.5-VL-3B SFT检查点出发，在匹配学生rollout和策略更新次数的公平对比下，FIRE-VLA在6,019个样本（150个保留nuScenes场景）上保持可比的单样本规划能力，将G=4平均L2从1.848降至1.500米，评估持续失败率从13.03%降至11.20%。误差降低主要源于罕见严重rollout的减少，而非普通轨迹的均匀改进。

Conclusion: FIRE-VLA验证了将强化学习中的"相对失败"转化为"绝对监督"的有效性，且通过同规模教师的自蒸馏即可实现，无需外部更大模型。其核心优势在于允许失败分布随策略动态变化，持续引导策略走出严重失败区域，尤其改善罕见但高风险的失控rollout。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning improves autonomous-driving vision-language-action (VLA) models by evaluating trajectories sampled from the current policy. Group relative policy optimization (GRPO) learns from reward differences within each rollout group. When all sampled trajectories are poor, this relative signal can rank failures without identifying behavior outside the failed region. We introduce FIRE-VLA, a failure-informed self-evolution framework that converts such unresolved failures into privileged supervision for the next policy. Low-reward, low-diversity groups trigger self-distillation from a frozen round-start copy of the same model. Teacher and student have the same parameter scale, but only the teacher observes the hidden future trajectory. Supervision follows the student's generated prefix and is restricted to answer tokens, while GRPO remains active for every group. The updated policy supplies the teacher for the next round, allowing the routed failure distribution to change with the policy without requiring a larger external teacher. Starting from the same Qwen2.5-VL-3B SFT checkpoint, the comparison matches student rollout and policy-update counts. On 6,019 examples from 150 held-out nuScenes scenes, FIRE-VLA retains comparable single-sample planning, reduces G=4 mean L2 from 1.848 to 1.500 m, and lowers evaluation-persistent failure prevalence from 13.03% to 11.20%. The reduction in mean error arises mainly from rare severe rollouts rather than uniform improvement across ordinary trajectories.

</details>


### [111] [Capstan-driven Continuum Surgical Robot: Design, Modeling, and Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13396)
*Gang Zhang,Yufu Qiu,Junyan Yan,Wenhui Zeng,Wenlong Lu,Shing Shin Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于"驱动-感知协同设计"（actuation-perception co-design）的集成设计-建模-传感框架，通过在电机安装支架中引入柔性元件实现实时缆绳张力测量，并结合多体短厚梁模型的并行计算框架与近端多轴力/力矩传感器，实现紧凑型绳驱连续体手术机器人的实时形状与力感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形状与力感知长期是紧凑型绳驱（capstan-driven）连续体手术机器人发展的关键瓶颈，主要难点在于在狭小的绞盘组件空间内难以获取缆绳张力信息。现有方法受限于空间约束，无法在不牺牲紧凑性的前提下实现集成感知。

Method: 1) 提出驱动-感知协同设计概念：在驱动系统的电机安装支架中引入柔性元件，使其在缆绳反作用力下产生微形变，从而在不占用紧凑绞盘空间的前提下实现实时缆绳张力测量。2) 针对空间缆绳布线策略带来的非传统关节构型建模复杂性，提出基于多体短厚梁模型的并行计算框架，捕捉短梁段的剪切效应及多缆绳协同交互，同时实现实时性能。3) 引入近端多轴力/力矩传感器作为额外测量锚点，结合上述框架实现稳定的形状与力感知。

Result: 基于该框架研制了单段和双段构型的绳驱连续体手术机器人。实验在单段和双段连续体机器人上均验证了框架的有效性，实现了实时尖端姿态估计以及接触力与接触位置的感知。

Conclusion: 该框架在不损害紧凑绞盘架构的前提下实现了缆绳张力反馈，使集成感知在绳驱连续体手术机器人中成为可行，为后续此类机器人的感知能力提升提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: Shape and force sensing have long been critical bottlenecks in the development of compact capstan-driven continuum surgical robots, primarily due to the difficulty of obtaining cable tension information within the confined capstan assembly. To overcome these challenges, this paper presents an integrated design-modeling-sensing approach based on the concept of actuation-perception co-design. A compliant element is introduced into the motor mounting bracket of the drive system, enabling micro-deformation under the cable reaction force and thereby allowing real-time cable tension measurement without occupying the compact capstan space. To address the modeling complexity arising from unconventional joint configurations introduced by the spatial cable routing strategy, a parallel computation framework based on a multibody short-thick-beam model is proposed, which captures shear effects in short beam segments and synergistic multi-cable interactions while achieving real-time performance. Building on this framework, stable shape and force sensing is achieved by incorporating a proximal multi-axis force/torque sensor as an additional measurement anchor. Following this design-modeling-sensing framework, capstan-driven continuum surgical robots with single- and dual-segment configurations are developed. Experimental results validate the proposed framework in both single- and dual-segment continuum robots, demonstrating real-time tip pose estimation together with contact force and location perception. By enabling cable tension feedback without compromising the compact capstan architecture, the proposed framework makes integrated perception feasible for capstan-driven continuum surgical robots.

</details>


### [112] [Deliberate Practice: Learning Robot Skills under a Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13415)
*Shivam Vats,Sudarshan Harithas,Mete Tuluhan Akbulut,Arvind Raghunathan,George Konidaris*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Deliberate Practice (DP)算法，用于在有限练习预算下自主学习机器人技能，通过双线性规划精确计算预算最优的技能练习分配方案，并在长时程操控任务中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顺序任务中的机器人技能自主学习面临有限的练习预算约束，需要决定哪些技能值得练习以及练习多久，以最大化任务的整体回报。现有方法缺乏系统性解决该分配问题的手段，特别是在需要考虑组合多个技能计划场景下，计算预算最优分配具有挑战性。

Method: 提出Deliberate Practice (DP)算法，估计两个关键量：掌握技能所需时间和技能所解锁任务计划的累积奖励。核心贡献是将预算最优分配问题建模为双线性规划（bilinear program），可使用现成的优化求解器精确求解，从而在组合性庞大的技能计划空间中进行推理。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界的长时程操控任务实验中，DP算法使机器人能够在有限的练习时间内最优地获取有用策略，并显著提升了长时程规划的性能。

Conclusion: 通过将技能练习分配建模为可精确求解的双线性规划问题，DP算法为有限预算下的自主技能学习提供了一种理论上可证明最优的解决方案，有效解决了长时程任务中资源分配的难题。

Abstract: We consider the problem of autonomously learning robot skills under a limited practice budget for sequential tasks. We propose an active skill learning algorithm, \emph{Deliberate Practice (DP)}, that computes a provably \emph{budget-optimal} allocation---practicing skills that maximize expected cumulative reward while being learnable within the budget. DP estimates both the time needed to master skills and the cumulative reward of the task plans that the skills unlock. Computing a budget-optimal allocation is challenging as it requires reasoning about combinatorially many skill plans over a large practice budget. Our key contribution is a bilinear program that can compute this exactly using off-the-shelf solvers. Through simulated and real-world experiments on long-horizon manipulation tasks, we show that our approach allows robots to optimally use limited practice time to acquire useful policies and improve long-horizon planning.

</details>


### [113] [Attention from Action, for Action: Emergent Visual Bottlenecks for Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13422)
*Zheyu Zhuang,Ruiyu Wang,Nick Heppert,Johannes Fabian Hahn,Abhinav Valada,Florian T. Pokorny,Danica Kragic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Seeker，一种任务和状态条件化的视觉注意力学习机制。通过从DINOv3冻结特征中迭代更新查询向量来汇聚视觉证据，仅靠动作监督即可产生随任务进展变化的ROI（感兴趣区域），用于RGB裁剪、掩码引导背景增强和点云过滤，在仿真和真实机器人上显著提升数据效率和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的ROI界面往往依赖外部空间标签（如注视点、物体类别或可供性标注），而免标签方法（如基于夹爪或运动事件居中裁剪）虽然无需额外标注，但编码了固定的时间选择、代理点和裁剪尺度，当控制所需的视觉证据位于末端执行器之外或随任务进展连续变化时，这些裁剪会错位。

Method: Seeker从冻结的DINOv3特征出发，通过迭代注意力机制（iterative query update）利用收集的视觉证据逐步更新查询向量，仅从动作监督中学习产生与任务进展相关的ROI。该学习到的ROI作为空间接口，用于RGB裁剪、掩码引导背景增强以及点云过滤。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界中，Seeker在数据效率和鲁棒性上均优于无裁剪、仅增强和动作派生裁剪的基线。在真实机器人上，域内成功率从最优基线的48.3%提升至76.7%；光照/背景变化下的成功率从20.0%提升至60.0%。

Conclusion: 通过从动作中学习注意力而非依赖外部标签或固定启发式规则，Seeker实现了随任务进展动态调整的可学习ROI，显著改善了视动学习的数据效率和分布偏移鲁棒性，验证了"任务与状态条件化注意力读取"在机器人操作学习中的有效性。

Abstract: Visual bottlenecks that focus policy inputs on regions of interest (ROIs) can improve data-efficient visuomotor learning by separating where to look from how to act. Many ROI interfaces rely on external spatial labels, such as gaze, object classes, or affordance annotations. Label-free alternatives often derive crops from trajectories by detecting gripper or motion events and centering a fixed crop at the projected end-effector. Such action-derived crops are useful spatial priors that require no additional labels, but they encode fixed choices about event timing, proxy points, and crop scale. When the visual evidence needed for control lies away from the end-effector or changes continuously with task progress, these crops can become misaligned. We propose Seeker, a task- and state-conditioned readout that learns attention from action. Starting from frozen DINOv3 features, Seeker iteratively updates a query with gathered visual evidence, producing progression-aware ROIs solely from action supervision. The learned ROI serves as a spatial interface for RGB cropping, mask-guided background augmentation, and point-cloud filtering. In simulation and the real world, Seeker improves data efficiency and robustness over no-crop, augmentation, and action-derived crop baselines. On real robots, Seeker raises average in-domain success from the best baseline's 48.3% to 76.7% and success under lighting/background shifts from 20.0% to 60.0%.

</details>


### [114] [ContactGuard: Pre-Contact Execution Monitoring with Action-Conditioned Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13438)
*Gehan Zheng,Matthew Johnson-Roberson,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ContactGuard，一个针对分块视觉运动策略的接触前执行监控器。它利用潜在世界模型在潜在视觉空间预测策略动作块的短期后果，并由轻量级失败探针判断是否失败，从而在接触发生前中止机器人行为，无需修改底层策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的操纵任务中，失败往往在机器人已接触物体后才被检测到。在腕部相机设置下，近距离手爪-物体视图有助于观察接触，但糟糕的接近动作可能在传统检测器反应前就已推动、错失、滑动或扰动物体，因此需要在接触发生前进行监控和干预。

Method: ContactGuard包含两部分：1) 潜在世界模型，从无标注机器人轨迹中训练，在规划动作下预测紧凑的多视图视觉嵌入的未来潜在状态，避免像素级视频预测；2) 轻量级失败探针，从少量标注的接触前片段中训练。部署时，在临近接触事件前锚定预测，用策略自身动作前向滚动模型，并验证预测的接触后潜在状态。

Result: 在真实世界接触丰富的操纵任务中，ContactGuard的失败预测准确率优于直接预测和损坏动作消融基线，并能作为接触前中止信号转移到实机机器人上，且无需修改底层策略。

Conclusion: ContactGuard通过潜在空间的世界模型和轻量级失败探针，实现了接触前的失败预测与中止，为接触丰富操纵任务提供了一种不侵入底层策略的有效安全监控方案。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation failures are often detected only after the robot has committed to contact. This is especially limiting in wrist-camera setups: close gripper--object views help observe contact, but a poor approach may already push, miss, slip, or disturb the object before conventional detectors react. We introduce \emph{ContactGuard}, a pre-contact execution monitor for chunked visuomotor policies. Given the policy's planned action chunk, ContactGuard predicts its short-horizon consequence in latent visual space and aborts if the predicted future latent indicates likely failure. Its latent world model is trained from unlabelled robot trajectories to predict compact multi-view visual embeddings under planned actions, avoiding pixel-level video prediction. A lightweight failure probe is then trained from a small labelled set of pre-contact clips. At deployment, ContactGuard anchors prediction before an imminent contact event, rolls the model forward under the policy's own actions, and verifies the predicted post-contact latent. Across real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks, ContactGuard predicts failure more accurately than direct and corrupted-action ablations, and transfers to live robot as a pre-contact abort signal without modifying the underlying policy.

</details>


### [115] [Mind the Context: Continual Learning of Socially Appropriate Robot Actions via Environmental-Social Disentanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13448)
*Rafal Robert Karpinski,Fethiye Irmak Dogan,Nikhil Churamani,Yiming Luo,Maartje M. A. de Graaf,Davide Dell'Anna,Hatice Gunes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了显式解耦双分支（EDD）框架，用于社交机器人在领域增量设置下的持续学习。EDD将环境知识与社交主体知识显式分离，并通过基于重放的排练机制缓解灾难性遗忘，在多个室内场景中学习适当机器人动作（如清洁、服务、发起对话）。实验表明EDD优于多个最先进基线，消融研究进一步评估了不同解耦策略和对领域顺序的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交机器人需在多样环境中运行，类似布局可能隐含不同的社会适当行为（如拥挤家庭中开启对话可接受，但会议中则具干扰性）。由于规范和环境无法预先全部预料，机器人需要通过持续学习从序列经验中适应，同时保持已有知识。先前工作未解决领域增量设置，即机器人逐步遇到不同上下文（如客厅、会议室、办公室、走廊）时，环境线索（空间开放或拥挤）和社交线索（人员或智能体位置）共同决定动作的适当性。

Method: 提出的EDD框架通过两条分支显式分离环境相关知识与社交主体相关知识，并利用基于重放的排练（replay-based rehearsal）机制在跨多个室内领域学习机器人动作适当性时缓解灾难性遗忘。该方法包含解耦策略设计，并通过消融实验评估不同解耦方式及领域顺序敏感性。

Result: 实验表明EDD在多个最先进基线上表现优越；消融研究进一步评估了不同的解耦策略和领域顺序的敏感性。代码已公开。

Conclusion: EDD框架有效解决了社交机器人在领域增量持续学习条件下的动作适当性学习问题，通过显式解耦环境与社交知识并结合重放机制，在多个室内领域中优于现有方法，为社交机器人在动态多变环境中的适应性提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Social robots are expected to operate across diverse environments, where similar arrangements can imply different socially appropriate actions, e.g., starting a conversation may be acceptable in a crowded home but disruptive in an office meeting. Because such norms and environments cannot all be anticipated in advance, robots require continual learning (CL) to adapt from sequential experience while retaining previously acquired knowledge. Prior work has studied CL for generating socially appropriate robot actions, but it has not addressed domain-incremental settings in which the robot incrementally encounters diverse contexts (e.g., living room, meeting room, office, hallway), where both environmental (e.g., whether the space is open or cluttered with furniture) and social cues (e.g., how people or other agents are positioned around the robot) jointly shape the appropriateness of robot actions. We address this gap with the Explicit Disentanglement Dual-Branch (EDD) framework. EDD explicitly separates environmental and social-agent related knowledge and uses replay-based rehearsal to mitigate forgetting while learning the appropriateness of robot actions (e.g., cleaning, serving, starting a conversation) across several indoor domains. Experiments show that EDD outperforms several state-of-the-art baselines, and ablation studies further evaluate different disentanglement strategies and the sensitivity to domain ordering. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Cambridge-AFAR/Mind-the-Context.git.

</details>


### [116] [Decoding Task Progress from VLA Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13474)
*Atiksh Bhardwaj,Edward Weiyi Duan,Prithwish Dan,Wei-Chiu Ma,Preston Culbertson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文利用机械可解释性方法探测π0.5视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）的残差流，发现任务进度（轨迹中归一化的剩余时间）可以从激活中线性读取。该信号在预训练的PaliGemma骨干网络中就存在，无需机器人数据训练。线性探针可泛化到未见任务，在多提示数据上训练时对语言反事实敏感，但不能用于策略引导。该探针可作为无标签OOD检测器检测停滞任务进度，性能与最先进方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型正快速走向通用操作策略部署，但目前缺乏理解这些模型内部表示或运行时监控的基本工具，需要可解释性和可监控性手段。

Method: 利用机械可解释性思想，对π0.5的残差流进行线性探测（linear probing），训练线性分类器/回归器来读取任务进度信号，并测试其在不同条件下的泛化能力及OOD检测用途。

Result: 任务进度可从激活中线性读取；该信号在预训练PaliGemma骨干中已存在；线性探针可泛化到未见任务，对语言反事实敏感但不能有效引导策略；作为无标签OOD检测器与最先进方法相当。

Conclusion: VLA具有丰富的、可线性读取的语义量（如任务进度）内部表示，学习读取这些信号为监控部署中的视觉运动策略提供了一条轻量级、可解释的路径。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs) are moving rapidly towards deployment as general-purpose manipulation policies, but we currently lack basic tools for understanding what these models represent internally or for monitoring them at runtime. Leveraging ideas from mechanistic interpretability, we probe the residual stream of $π_{0.5}$ and find that task progress, the normalized time remaining in a trajectory, is linearly readable from the activations. We find that this signal is present in the pretrained PaliGemma backbone prior to training on any robot-specific data. A single linear probe generalizes to unseen tasks and varies under language counterfactuals when trained on multi-prompt data, but does not enable meaningful steering of the policy. These properties make the signal directly useful for instrumenting deployed VLAs. We use the probe as a simple label-free OOD detector, which detects stalled task progress, and find it competitive with state-of-the-art methods. Our results suggest that VLAs have rich, linearly readable internal representations of semantic quantities like task progress, and that learning to read these signals offers a lightweight, interpretable path toward monitoring deployed visuomotor policies.

</details>


### [117] [A Browser-Native Digital Test Range for Benchmarking 4D Ocean-Glider Planning Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13511)
*Edward Holmberg,Elias Ioup,Mahdi Abdelguerfi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个浏览器原生、免安装的海洋滑翔机规划器数字测试场，它通过统一的"规划到观测"契约将人工路线、内置算法和外部导入的学习型/经典规划器整合到可复现的四维实验中，并经OSSE实验和真实部署验证了其科学可追溯性与组件合格性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重复地对海洋滑翔机规划器进行实地评估需要稀缺的载具、操作员、布放与回收资源，且海洋条件无法为竞争算法重置，这严重阻碍了规划器的系统性对比与验证。需要一个可复现、低成本、无安装门槛的测试环境来解决这一难题。

Method: 构建了一个引导式、浏览器原生（免安装）的数字测试场，涵盖从区域域选择、任务级测深、时间/深度强迫、科学目标设定、可选任务分解、路径规定、海流平流执行、观测生成到评分全流程。核心创新是统一的"规划到观测"契约，将载具、感知与评估假设统一起来；同时通过Pyodide/WebAssembly实现源码锁定的GliderFlight 1.2.0原生到浏览器的等价性。

Result: 在受控OSSE实验中，以5个经典规划器、2个片段、3个确定性种子和校准的60小时视界进行了评估；全部54个任务均完成并恢复，无硬性违规，规划器排名与潜深策略效应揭示了运行-科学权衡。公开部署提供了现场参照审计以界定当前运动学边界，GliderFlight 1.2.0实现了原生到浏览器的等价性。

Conclusion: 该系统提供的运行空间在科学上可追溯且组件合格，适用于任务级部署前的预部署实验，为海洋滑翔机规划器提供了一种可重复、可审计的替代实海测试方案。

Abstract: Repeated in-situ evaluation of ocean-glider planners requires scarce vehicles, operators, deployment and recovery resources, and ocean conditions that cannot be reset for competing algorithms. We present a guided, installation-free browser-native digital test range that transforms a selected region into a reproducible four-dimensional experiment. The system leads users from regional domain selection through mission-scoped bathymetry, time/depth forcing, science objectives, optional task decomposition, route specification, current-advected execution, observation generation, and scoring. Its primary contribution is a common plan-to-observation contract unifying vehicle, sensing, and evaluator assumptions across manual routes, transparent built-in algorithms, and imported classical or learned-planner outputs, while exported artifacts form dataset-ready records. A controlled Observing System Simulation Experiment (OSSE) evaluates five classical planners in two episodes, three deterministic seeds, and a calibrated 60-hour horizon. All 54 missions completed and recovered without hard violations, while planner rankings and dive-policy effects revealed operational-scientific tradeoffs. An authentic public deployment supplied a field-referenced audit to scope current kinematic boundaries. Separately, source-locked GliderFlight 1.2.0 achieved native-to-browser parity through Pyodide/WebAssembly, establishing a pathway for high-fidelity multi-tier simulation. The resulting operational space is scientifically traceable and component-qualified for mission-scale pre-deployment experimentation.

</details>


### [118] [HumanTracker: Towards Comprehensive and Human-Aligned Motion Tracking Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13555)
*Dairu Liu,Zekun Qi,Jiayu Zeng,Ruixi Yu,Yu Guan,Yintianrun Zhang,Xuchuan Chen,Sikai Liang,Zekai Li,Chenghuai Lin,Xinqiang Yu,Wenyao Zhang,He Wang,Li Yi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 HumanTracker 基准与 HumanScore 指标，用于改进人形机器人运动跟踪评估。基准包含约153小时光学运动轨迹，覆盖四种运动族并带文本标签；HumanScore 是一种基于偏好对齐的训练指标（12K 运动对/24K 运动），相比传统运动学误差更能预测人类感知偏好，并揭示接触与稳定性故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人形运动跟踪评估使用逐帧运动学误差（如平均姿态差异），与实际人类在视频中感知的质量不一致——忽略了不稳定支撑、脚部滑移、错误触地时刻等物理伪影。此外，现有测试集规模小、多样性不足，难以覆盖接触密集和长时程行为。

Method: 构建 HumanTracker 基准（约153小时多专业表演者的光学运动轨迹，分为四种运动族并配文本标签用于细粒度诊断）；提出 HumanScore 指标，在12K运动对（含24K运动）上训练，使其与人类偏好对齐。

Result: 在多种代表性最先进跟踪器上，HumanScore 相比运动学指标更能预测人类偏好，并能揭示传统运动学误差常常遗漏的接触与稳定性失败。

Conclusion: HumanTracker 与 HumanScore 使人体运动跟踪评估既与人类感知对齐又具可扩展性，弥补了现有运动学指标在物理真实性评估上的不足。

Abstract: Humanoid motion tracking is central to teleoperation and whole-body imitation, yet evaluation often disagrees with what people perceive in videos. Kinematic errors average per-frame pose differences but miss the physical artifacts that matter most, particularly unstable support and incorrect contacts such as foot skating and mistimed touch-downs. Meanwhile, widely used test suites are small and lack the diversity needed to stress contact-rich, long-horizon behaviors. We introduce HumanTracker to make humanoid tracking evaluation both perceptually aligned and scalable. The HumanTracker benchmark contains approximately 153 hours of optical motion trajectories from multiple professional performers, organized into four motion families with text labels for fine-grained diagnosis. We further propose HumanScore, a preference-aligned metric trained on 12K motion pairs containing 24K motions. Across representative state-of-the-art trackers, HumanScore better predicts human preferences and reveals contact and stability failures that kinematic metrics often miss.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [Position: Reasoning is a Learnable Rule-Based Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12325)
*Rachel Lawrence,Jacqueline Maasch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场性论文（position paper），指出当前生成式AI社区在"推理"（reasoning）定义上的模糊性导致推理评估的构念效度（construct validity）无法验证，进而阻碍了可信自主推理的可量化进展。作者提出基于文献综合的操作性定义（将有效且健全的推理定位为可学习的基于规则的过程），并提供一份关于AI推理研究沟通最佳实践的检查清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI推理研究进展迅速，但生成式AI社区未能就"推理"的操作性定义达成共识，且往往隐含地排斥逻辑学和可验证自动推理中的历史性处理方式。这种定义性模糊使推理评估的构念效度无法验证，导致无法量化地衡量迈向可信自主推理的进展。

Method: 作者采用立场论述的方法：从文献综合角度提出操作性定义，将有效（valid）和健全（sound）的推理定位为一种可学习的、基于规则的（rule-based）过程；同时提供一份用于规范AI推理研究沟通的最佳实践检查清单。

Result: 论文提供了两项核心产出：(1) 基于文献综合的操作性定义，将推理视为可学习的规则驱动过程；(2) 一套AI推理研究沟通最佳实践检查清单，用于提高研究透明度与可比性。

Conclusion: 作者认为推理定义模糊这一问题是可以解决的，通过明确的操作性定义和沟通规范，可以恢复推理评估的构念效度，从而促进可量化、可信赖的自主推理研究的进展。

Abstract: Autonomous reasoning is among the most scientifically and economically motivating topics in AI today. Historically the purview of symbolic AI, recent advances have mainly emerged from deep probabilistic generative models. Despite immense interest and rapid progress, the generative AI community has not clearly converged on operational definitions for reasoning and often implicitly rejects the historical treatment of this topic in logic and verifiable automated reasoning. This position contends that definitional ambiguity leaves the construct validity of reasoning evaluation unverifiable, undermining quantifiable progress toward trustworthy autonomous reasoning. We also contend that this ambiguity is addressable. To that end, we provide (1) operational definitions based on a synthesis of the literature, positioning valid and sound reasoning as a learnable rule-based process; and (2) a checklist for best practices in the communication of AI reasoning research.

</details>


### [120] [Diagnostic Foundation for Evaluating LLMs' Research Integrity as Co-Scientists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12345)
*Yash Tripathi,Silu Sharma,Sai Sidhanth Manoharan Jayanthi,Shivank Garg,Lin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出IntegrityBench基准，评估语言模型在机构压力下维护研究诚信的能力，涵盖36对任务和5级压力协议，发现前沿模型在峰值压力下约1/3的诚信关键决策出错，且模型规模和推理能力无法可靠缓解这一缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型越来越多地被部署为"共同科学家"，但其在机构压力下维护研究诚信的能力尚未被测量。现有评估未考虑研究场景中特有的制度性压力（如发表压力、利益冲突等）对模型诚信决策的影响，亟需一个专门基准来量化这一风险。

Method: 构建IntegrityBench基准，包含三个维度（不当行为分类、伦理行为推理、基于工件的情境决策）的36对任务，采用5级显式-隐式压力协议，跨越3个领域和4个研究阶段，对18个前沿模型变体进行系统评估。

Result: 在峰值压力下，模型在约1/3的诚信关键决策中失败；显式压力导致模型顺从不当行为，而隐式情境重构更多导致对合法研究任务的过度拒绝；有趣的是，在不当行为分类上表现差的模型在工件基决策上表现相当或更好（85.7 vs 79.4），表明三个维度结构性地分离，正确的伦理行为并不需要准确的分类能力。

Conclusion: 前沿模型可能表面上表现出色，但实则存在诚信失败，产生两种不同的部署风险：助长研究不当行为（如数据篡改、抄袭）以及侵蚀对AI辅助研究的信任。模型规模与推理能力并不能可靠地缓解这些诚信风险，暗示需要专门的诚信对齐机制。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly deployed as co-scientists, yet their ability to uphold research integrity under institutional pressure remains unmeasured. We introduce IntegrityBench, a benchmark evaluating misconduct classification, ethical action reasoning and artifact-grounded decision making across 36 paired tasks under a 5-level implicit-explicit pressure protocol spanning 3 domains and 4 research stages. Evaluating 18 frontier model variants, we find that under peak pressure, models fail roughly 1 in 3 integrity-critical decisions, and neither scale nor reasoning ability reliably mitigates this. Explicit pressures induce compliance with misconduct, while implicit contextual reframing more often causes over-refusal of legitimate research tasks. Interestingly, models failing to classify research requests accurately perform equally or better on artifact-grounded decision making (85.7 vs. 79.4), suggesting the three facets are structurally dissociated and correct ethical action does not require accurate classification. Frontier models can thus appear helpful while harbouring integrity failures that create two distinct deployment risks: facilitating research misconduct and eroding trust in AI-assisted research.

</details>


### [121] [Position: The Alignment Community is Unintentionally Building a Censor's Toolkit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12346)
*Sarah Ball,Phil Hackemann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文认为现代AI对齐方法原本用于防止有害输出，但他们是具有双重用途的技术，可能被恶意行为者滥用于审查和操纵。作者呼吁社区正视这一双重用途风险并制定缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI作为信息提供者被快速采用、经济力量不对称以及政治格局趋向威权主义，AI对齐机制（原本用于安全）可能被恶意利用为信息控制的工具，这一风险亟待讨论。

Method: 作为立场论文，通过将现有对齐技术映射到可能的滥用场景和实际案例，论证对齐方法作为"双用途技术"的可能性和现实性。

Result: 表明追求"完美对齐"模型的过程，客观上为恶意行为者提供了不断提升的信息主导工具，并凸显了这一双重用途风险的紧迫性。

Conclusion: 呼吁社区关注AI对齐机制的故意滥用，并提出缓解策略以防范其双重用途潜力带来的风险。

Abstract: This position paper argues that modern AI alignment methods - originally designed to prevent harmful output - are dual-use technologies that may easily be misused by malicious actors for censorship and manipulation. By mapping current alignment techniques to the possibility and actual cases of misuse, we show that the quest for a "perfectly aligned" model inadvertently also provides malicious actors with an ever-improving tool for informational dominance. We need to discuss this dual-use potential now, as its risk is exacerbated by rapid user adoption of AI as information provider, economic power asymmetries, and a political landscape that increasingly shifts towards authoritarianism. We conclude by urging the community to consider the intentional misuse of AI alignment mechanisms and propose mitigation strategies to safeguard against this dual-use potential.

</details>


### [122] [Agreement Is Not Alignment: Divergent Moral Grounds in Human and LLM Ethical Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12368)
*Octavian M. Machidon,Alina L. Machidon,Vojko Strahovnik,Mateja Centa Strahovnik,Jonas Miklavčič,Marko Robnik Šikonja*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过一个包含五个道德判断领域、500个条目的ETHICS基准测试，比较了人类标注者和LLM在最终标签与支持性理由两个层面的一致性，发现尽管标签层面的一致率很高，但理由层面的分析揭示了人类与模型在道德依据上的系统性分歧，表明“一致性”不等于“对齐”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常用“与人类判断一致”作为评估LLM对齐的代理指标，但最终标签的一致并不能证明人类与模型基于相同的道德原则、情境假设或解释推理。两个主体可能在结论上一致，却依赖完全不同的道德依据。因此需要区分“标签一致性”与“理由一致性”，并检验模型是否真的在道德推理层面上与人类对齐。

Method: 构建一个基于ETHICS衍生数据集、包含500个条目的基准测试，覆盖五个道德判断领域。收集人类标注者与多种LLM（涵盖前沿和开源模型系列）对最终标签和支撑理由的标注。通过标签层面的多数一致性评估，以及理由层面的定量和定性分析，比较人类与模型所依赖的道德范畴（如伤害、尊重、守信、公正、应得、免责相关性）分布的异同。

Result: 在标签层面，多种模型与人类标注者多数意见的一致率通常很高；然而在理由层面，模型与人类标注者在道德依据上表现出系统性分歧，特别是在不同道德范畴上的注意力分配有明显差异，即使最终标签与人类多数判断一致时也是如此。

Conclusion: 研究表明标签一致性不能等同于模型与人类的道德对齐。仅基于标签的评估可能产生误导性的安全感，必须辅以对模型判断中所体现的理由、原则和道德优先级的分析，才能更可靠地评估LLM的对齐程度。

Abstract: Agreement with human judgments is a common proxy for evaluating the alignment of large language models (LLMs). Yet agreement in final labels does not show that human annotators and models rely on the same moral grounds. Two agents may reach the same judgment while appealing to different principles, contextual assumptions, or interpretations of the situation.
  We test this distinction using a curated 500-item ETHICS-derived benchmark spanning five domains of moral judgment, with new human annotator and LLM annotations of both final labels and supporting rationales. Across frontier and open model families, agreement with human annotator majority labels is often high. However, rationale-level analysis reveals systematic divergence in the moral grounds expressed by human annotators and models. In particular, models redistribute attention across categories such as harm, respect, promise-keeping, justice, desert, and excuse relevance, even when their final labels match the human annotator majority.
  Our results show that agreement should not be treated as equivalent to alignment. Label-based evaluation can therefore be misleadingly reassuring unless complemented by analysis of the reasons, principles, and moral priorities expressed in model judgments.

</details>


### [123] [Multi-Agent Scheduling with LLM-Assisted Contract Net Negotiation for Stream Processing in Mobile Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12371)
*Sabeur Lajili,Zaki Brahmi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MAS-DecStream系统，其核心贡献为LLM-MR-CNP协议——将经典合同网协议扩展为包含语义CFP制定、渐进式上下文披露、多轮提案修订、协商记忆和确定性验证的协商机制，用于异构移动边缘-云基础设施中的流处理调度。实验表明该方案可将延迟违规降至3%，消除资源过度承诺，在20个代理下冲突解决率达0.91，效用比多轮规则基线提升最多22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流处理系统在异构移动边缘-云基础设施中运行时面临工作负载波动、资源竞争和严格QoS要求的挑战，这使得去中心化调度变得复杂，需要更智能的协商机制来解决这些问题。

Method: 提出LLM-MR-CNP协议，通过以下机制扩展经典合同网协议：(1)语义化的调用提案(CFP)制定；(2)渐进式上下文披露；(3)多轮提案修订；(4)协商记忆；(5)确定性验证。边缘集群代理基于本地观测、预测资源状态和定性运行时上下文精炼自然语言卸载提案，同时保持硬性资源和QoS约束的确定性。

Result: 基于阿里巴巴ASI Trace的实验表明：延迟违规降至3%，资源过度承诺被消除，20个代理下冲突解决率为0.91，效用较多轮规则基线提升最多22%。25个案例的评估显示模型和提示词相关的准确率-成本权衡。多轮CNP精炼是协议层面主要增益来源，LLM辅助对定性和不确定性运行时上下文有价值。

Conclusion: 多轮合同网协议精炼是协议层面提升的主要驱动因素，而LLM辅助在处理定性和不确定的运行时上下文方面发挥补充价值，为LLM与传统协商协议结合提供了初步证据。

Abstract: Stream-processing systems increasingly operate across heterogeneous mobile edge--cloud infrastructures, where workload volatility, resource contention, and stringent quality-of-service (QoS) requirements complicate decentralized scheduling. This paper proposes \emph{MAS-DecStream}, whose main contribution is \emph{LLM-MR-CNP}: an extension of the classical Contract Net Protocol with semantic CFP formulation, progressive context disclosure, multi-round proposal revision, negotiation memory, and deterministic validation. Edge-cluster agents refine natural-language offloading proposals from local observations, predicted resource states, and qualitative runtime context, while hard resource and QoS constraints remain deterministic. Experiments derived from the Alibaba ASI Trace evaluate the extension at three levels: single- versus multi-round CNP, rule-based versus LLM-assisted refinement, and fixed-model single- versus multi-round negotiation. Under the evaluated configurations, MAS-DecStream reduces latency violations to 3\%, eliminates resource overcommitment, reaches a conflict-resolution rate of 0.91 with 20 agents, and improves utility by up to 22\% over the multi-round rule-based baseline. A separate 25-case evaluation shows model- and prompt-dependent accuracy--cost trade-offs. The results provide initial evidence that multi-round CNP refinement is the principal protocol-level gain, with LLM assistance adding value for qualitative and uncertain runtime context.

</details>


### [124] [Position: We Need Practical AI Alignment Methods to Mirror Human Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12372)
*Vijay Keswani,Breanna K. Nguyen,Cyrus Cousins,Vincent Conitzer,Walter Sinnott-Armstrong,Jana Schaich Borg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"认知对齐"（cognitive alignment）概念，主张在关键决策场景中，AI系统不仅需要行为对齐，更需要在推理方式上与用户相似，并忠实传达其推理过程。通过综述证据和新调查数据，论证认知对齐对可理解性与可信度的重要性，并指出当前对齐方法的不足，提出相应的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI越来越多地作为决策辅助、决策委托或自主决策者使用，在关键决策场景中，用户不仅需要AI给出正确结果，还需要理解AI的推理过程。作者认为，仅靠现有的行为对齐（behavioral alignment）不足以满足这种需求，认知对齐的缺失可能成为AI应用落地的重大障碍。

Method: 本文为立场论文（position paper），采用三种方法：(1) 系统综述已有证据，说明认知对齐如何提升可理解性和可信度；(2) 提供新的调查数据，证明许多用户当AI的推理依据对其重要时，会将认知对齐视为"必不可少"；(3) 分析现有对齐方法与认知对齐需求之间的差距，提出研究议程。

Result: 提供了认知对齐能改善可理解性与可信度的证据综述；新调查数据表明许多用户将认知对齐视为"必要"；指出当前对齐方法与实现认知对齐之间的关键差距；提出了一项应对这些差距的研究议程。

Conclusion: 认知错位可能成为AI在众多预期应用中落地的阻碍。解决认知对齐问题是创建用户既愿意依赖、也有理由依赖的AI系统的关键，需要在推理相似性与忠实沟通推理两个方面同时推进。

Abstract: AI systems are increasingly employed as decision aids, decision delegates, or autonomous decision-makers. This position paper argues that in many settings, particularly high-stakes decision-making, we need accurate cognitively-aligned AI systems that reason similarly to their users, and faithfully communicate their reasoning. We review evidence that cognitive alignment improves understandability and trustworthiness, and provide new survey data showing that many users find cognitive alignment "essential" when an AI's rationale for a judgment or action is important to them. We outline the gaps between existing alignment methods and what is needed to achieve cognitive alignment, and present a research agenda to address these gaps. We argue that cognitive misalignment represents a likely impediment to AI adoption in many envisioned applications, and that addressing it is important for creating AI systems on which users are both willing and justified to rely.

</details>


### [125] [Don't Want Your LLM to Recommend Nuclear Strike? Try Asking It in Japanese](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12373)
*Rian Touchent*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 大型语言模型的安全对齐通常仅以英语评估，本文测试了九种模型在核打击高利害场景中是否受提示语言影响。结果发现日语提示显著降低Claude系列和Gemini Pro 3.1模型的发射率，且机制是"模型被要求用日语推理"而非输入语言本身——推理语言触发了模型自发生成英语提示中完全缺失的道德词汇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型越来越多地用于战略和咨询场景，但其安全对齐通常在英语环境下评估。现有研究忽视了多语言环境下模型安全行为可能不一致的问题，可能导致仅用英语评估而遗漏其他语言中编码的风险或安全机制。

Method: 使用单轮博弈论小场景（vignette），让模型向核武器国家建议是否打击无防御对手。提示刻意设计为非道德性且跨语言策略上等同。测试来自六家提供商的九种模型，并设计了跨语言实验：在英语提示中要求模型用日语推理，以隔离推理语言与输入语言的影响。

Result: 日语提示使Claude Sonnet 4.6在非必要打击场景中发射率从40%降至0%，在争议场景中从93%降至17%，但战略合理场景下几乎无影响；Gemini Pro 3.1从53%降至13%。当英语提示要求用日语推理时，发射率从93%降至37%，证明是推理语言而非输入语言驱动效果。日语推理时模型自发产生"道德成本""数百万生命"等提示中完全缺失的词汇。其他五种模型无语言效应，但在几乎所有条件下都倾向发射。

Conclusion: LLM的安全行为具有语言依赖性，仅用英语评估会遗漏其他语言中编码的风险和防护机制。语言效应仅在模型本身已在英语中表现出犹豫时才会出现，说明多语言安全评估对理解和保障LLM在高利害决策中的行为至关重要。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used in strategic and advisory contexts, yet their safety alignment is typically evaluated in English only. We test nine models from six providers and ask whether the language of a prompt can change a model's decision in a high-stakes scenario. We use single-turn game-theoretic vignettes in which a model advises a nuclear-armed nation on whether to strike a defenseless opponent. The prompt is intentionally amoral and strategically identical across languages. We find that Japanese prompts reduce launch rates in the Claude model family: Claude Sonnet 4.6 drops from 40% to 0% in scenarios where the strike is unnecessary and from 93% to 17% in contested scenarios, with minimal effect when the strike is strategically rational. The effect extends to Gemini Pro 3.1 (53% to 13%). A cross-language experiment isolates the mechanism: when instructed to reason in Japanese in an English prompt, launch rates drop from 93% to 37%. It is the language the model is asked to reason in, not the language of the input, that drives the effect. When reasoning in Japanese, models spontaneously generate moral vocabulary (''moral cost'', ''millions of lives'') that is entirely absent from the prompt. Five other models show no language effect, but they launch in nearly every condition regardless of language. The effect requires a model that already hesitates in English. These results show that LLM safety behavior is language-dependent, and that evaluating in English alone can miss both risks and safeguards encoded in other languages.

</details>


### [126] [Dual-Flow Transformers: Decoupling the Primary Prefill Path from Additional Decode Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12385)
*Liming Liu,Mingze Wang,Tuo Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Dual-Flow Transformer，一种双流架构，在保持主流程（处理prompt并写入KV cache）不变的同时，引入一个仅在解码阶段（从最后一个prompt位置开始）激活的辅助流，用于增加续写预测的计算量而不带来持续的KV状态开销。通过共享权重与KV cache，辅流在计算增量可控的情况下降低验证损失，并在MoE模型中实现了prefill与decode阶段专家计算量的独立控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的推理阶段（prefill和decode）对硬件有着不同的压力：prefill是并行且计算密集，decode是串行且受内存带宽限制。传统的宽度或深度扩展会同时增加这两个阶段的成本，因为每一层都要在两个阶段中被评估。作者希望找到一种方式，将额外的学习计算仅分配给续写预测，同时保留prompt-wide的主计算和单一持久的KV cache，从而在不线性增加两个阶段成本的前提下提升生成质量。

Method: Dual-Flow Transformer包含两条流：主流程是完整的因果语言模型，处理prompt并写入KV cache；辅助流在prompt处理阶段被省略，仅从最终的prompt位置开始激活，用于增加续写预测的计算，不写入持久状态也不影响主流程。两条流共享主要的attention、MLP和输出矩阵，但使用独立的token嵌入和轻量耦合。在MoE模型中，主辅流的专家fan-out可以独立控制，允许独立调节prefill成本、续写成本和预测质量。

Result: 在匹配token数的对比实验中，Dual-Flow在多种架构和数据配置下实现了更低的验证损失。在MoE模型中，作者研究了两种机制：(1)在固定prefill专家计算量的情况下增加decode计算；(2)在固定decode专家预算下重新分配两条流的计算。实验揭示了prefill-decode质量权衡，并展示了阶段特定专家分配的潜力。

Conclusion: Dual-Flow Transformer证明了将额外计算定向分配到decode阶段而非均匀扩展全模型，是一种有效的策略。共享权重和主缓存还创造了在执行过程中复用加载权重和缓存键值的可能性。该工作为推理成本优化提供了新的方向，特别是MoE模型中阶段特定的专家分配为推理效率与质量之间的权衡提供了更精细的控制手段。

Abstract: As large language models serve more requests, cumulative inference cost is becoming increasingly important relative to one-time training cost. The two inference phases stress hardware differently: prompt prefill is parallel and typically compute-bound, whereas autoregressive decode is sequential and often memory-bandwidth-bound. Conventional width or depth scaling increases both costs together because every added layer is evaluated in both phases. We ask whether additional learned computation can instead be allocated to continuation prediction while preserving the prompt-wide primary computation and a single persistent key-value (KV) cache. We introduce the Dual-Flow Transformer. Its primary flow is a complete causal language model that processes the prompt and writes the KV cache. The auxiliary flow is omitted during prompt processing and activated only from the final prompt position onward, adding continuation-prediction computation without writing persistent state or influencing the primary flow. The two flows share major attention, MLP, and output matrices, while using separate token embeddings and lightweight coupling. Sharing weights and the primary cache also creates opportunities to reuse loaded weights and cached keys and values during grouped execution. Across matched-token comparisons, Dual-Flow achieves lower validation loss across architectures and data configurations. In MoE models, the separation makes primary and auxiliary expert fan-outs independent controls over prompt cost, continuation cost, and predictive quality. We study two regimes: increasing decode computation at fixed prefill expert computation, and reallocating a fixed decode expert budget between the two flows. These experiments expose a prefill-decode-quality trade-off and demonstrate the potential of phase-specific expert allocation.

</details>


### [127] [Learning to Adapt Cross-Domain Preferences via Meta-LoRA for LLM Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12389)
*Xuefei Wang,Jun Han,Zixuan Wang,Qingkai Zeng,Xiao Wang,Ruijie Wang,Jianxin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PAC-Bayes正则化的Meta-LoRA方法，用于跨域零样本/少样本个性化对话生成。通过元学习的LoRA初始化作为起点和先验中心，依据支持集大小和预测不确定性调整更新强度，防止稀疏证据下的过拟合。同时将个性化先验功能分解为用户组件（可读提示）和领域组件（拓扑保持软token），在HiCUPID基准上显著提升跨域胜率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨域个性化方法面临两大问题：一是自适应方法在稀疏证据下难以校准更新幅度导致过拟合；二是历史迁移方法将用户偏好与源域伪影纠缠，产生不可靠的个性化先验和负迁移。核心动机是依据证据质量校准适应程度，并解耦先验中的用户与领域因素。

Method: 1) PAC-Bayes正则化的Meta-LoRA：将元学习的LoRA初始化同时用作适应起点和先验中心，根据支持集大小和预测不确定性动态调整更新强度——稀疏或模糊证据时限制更新，证据充足时允许更强个性化。2) 先验功能分解：将个性化先验拆分为用户组件（人类可读提示，表达稳定偏好）和领域组件（拓扑保持soft token，用于领域特定的隐空间条件化）。

Result: 在多个基准和个性化任务上一致优于强基线。在HiCUPID上，跨域胜率退化降低47.9%（相对最强竞争基线），未见用户冷启动场景下胜率提升110.2%。

Conclusion: 通过PAC-Bayes正则化控制适应幅度配合先验的功能性分解，能够在证据稀缺的跨域场景下实现可靠的个性化生成，避免过拟合与负迁移，显著优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: Cross-domain zero- or few-shot personalization aims to generate user-preferred responses in unseen conversational domains from only a handful of target-domain interactions. Existing adaptation methods struggle to calibrate update magnitude under sparse evidence and thus overfit, whereas history-transfer methods often entangle user preferences with source-domain artifacts, yielding unreliable personalization priors and negative transfer. To calibrate adaptation to evidence quality, we propose PAC-Bayes-regularized Meta-LoRA, which uses a meta-learned LoRA initialization as both the adaptation start and prior center, while adjusting update strength according to support-set size and predictive uncertainty. This limits overfitting under sparse or ambiguous evidence while permitting stronger personalization as evidence grows. Controlled adaptation alone does not determine which preferences should transfer across domains or how they should be expressed. We therefore functionally decompose personalization priors into user and domain components, using a human-readable prompt for stable preferences and topology-preserving soft tokens for domain-specific hidden-space conditioning. Experiments across multiple benchmarks and personalization tasks show consistent gains over strong baselines. On HiCUPID, our method reduces cross-domain win-rate degradation by 47.9% relative to the best competing baseline and improves win rate by 110.2% under unseen-user cold start.

</details>


### [128] [Research Assistant: AstraZeneca's Agentic System for R&D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12395)
*Piotr Grabowski,Mohamed Alameen,Jorge Bretones,Sabina Cardell,Miguel Carmona,Gavin Edwards,Ben Grainger,Sameh Hassan,Erik Jansson,Artur Kuziakhmetov,Albert Maristany,Hebatallah Mohamed,Andriy Nikolov,Sebastian Nilsson,Mark O'Donoghue,James Pacileo,Ashiq Sultan,Alex Voegele,Michael Ughetto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了阿斯利康内部开发的基于LLM的研究助手系统（Research Assistant），该系统通过聊天式界面整合科学文献、知识图谱、化学、临床试验、安全资源、表达数据和内部实验系统等多源数据，支持快速问答模式和多步骤复杂研究任务模式，响应均基于检索证据并链接回原始来源，为科学家和临床研究人员日常研发工作流提供规模化支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帮助科学家和临床研究人员在多样化的生物医学数据源中高效探索问题，应对跨多个异构数据源（文献、知识图谱、化学、临床试验等）进行综合检索与推理的挑战。

Method: 构建基于LLM的聊天式系统，整合多类生物医学数据源；设计快速模式（直接问答）和多步骤模式（复杂研究任务）两种交互方式；采用检索证据对响应进行grounding，并将回答链接回原始数据源以支持用户回溯和深入探索。

Result: 系统已在阿斯利康内部实现规模化部署，成功支持日常研发工作流。

Conclusion: 通过该技术说明，作者概述了系统架构、核心设计决策，以及在规模化部署中获得的经验教训，为类似企业内部LLM驱动的科研辅助系统提供了参考。

Abstract: We describe Research Assistant, an internal LLM-based system developed at AstraZeneca to help scientists and clinicians explore biomedical questions across a broad range of data sources. The system provides a chat-style interface that brings together evidence from scientific literature, knowledge graphs, chemistry, clinical trials, safety resources, expression data, and internal experimental systems. It supports both a fast mode for direct question answering and a multi-step mode for more complex research tasks. Responses are grounded in retrieved evidence and linked back to the original sources, allowing users to review and further explore the underlying data. In this technical note, we outline the system architecture, the main design choices behind the product, and lessons learned from deploying it at scale to support day-to-day R&D workflows across AstraZeneca.

</details>


### [129] [Large Language Models Can Follow Instructions, But Not Many at Once: Phase Transitions in Compositional Constraint Satisfaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12426)
*Mariya I. Vasileva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出约束饱和评估（CSE），一个程序化生成的基准，系统性地测试大语言模型在同时满足多个显式约束时的表现退化情况。通过对15个模型、36种约束类型、369,753次检查（k=1到12）的评估，发现模型在满足所有约束上的成功率随约束数量急剧崩溃（单个约束41%通过率的模型在8个约束下仅5.7%全通过），且约束退化不均（结构性约束比词汇性约束退化快2倍），失败几乎独立导致累积呈乘法效应。模型在超过5-6个约束后可靠指令遵循能力显著崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型被广泛应用于需要同时满足多种显式约束的场景（推理结构、安全边界、输出格式），但组合约束情境下的性能退化特征尚未被系统刻画。作者旨在回答三个问题：性能退化速率如何、退化由什么因素决定、能否缓解这种崩溃。

Method: 提出了程序化生成的基准CSE（Constraint Saturation Evaluation），系统性地改变同时约束数量k（1到12），每条约束由确定性规则验证器评分，完全排除LLM作为裁判的干扰。共测试15个模型、36种约束类型、369,753次检查。

Result: 三个主要发现：（1）单约束通过率逐步可预测地衰减，但同时满足所有k个约束的概率呈崩溃式下降——k=8时单约束通过约41%的模型全部成功仅5.7%；（2）约束退化不平等：结构性约束比词汇性约束每增加一个约束多损失2倍的基线能力，差异源于"理解-维持差距"（需要持续追踪的约束与免疫组合的二元决策约束之别）；（3）失败近乎独立，使累积呈乘法效应；残余耦合与共享输出特征相关而非成对干扰。可靠指令遵循在超过5-6个约束时崩溃：最强模型在7个约束时探测级成功率低于50%，15个模型中有12个在3个或更少约束时即跌破该阈值。

Conclusion: 组合约束下的指令遵循存在明确的容量极限（约5-6个约束），其崩溃机制主要由约束独立的失败累积（乘法效应）驱动，不同约束类型的退化差异源于其对持续理解-维持的需求差异。这一发现为理解LLM组合指令遵循瓶颈及可能的缓解策略提供了系统化的实证基础。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed in settings that require simultaneous adherence to multiple explicit constraints - reasoning structure, safety boundaries, output schemas. Individual constraints are handled proficiently, but the compositional regime, where many must hold jointly, remains poorly characterized: how rapidly does performance degrade, what governs the degradation, and can the collapse be mitigated? We introduce Constraint Saturation Evaluation (CSE), a procedurally generated benchmark that systematically varies the number of simultaneous constraints (k), with every constraint scored by a deterministic, rule-based verifier and zero LLM-judge involvement: 15 models, 36 constraint types, 369,753 checks at k=1-12. Three findings emerge. First, per-constraint pass rate decays gradually and predictably, while the chance of satisfying all k constraints collapses - a model passing individual constraints at ~41% at k=8 succeeds on all eight just 5.7% of the time. Second, constraints do not degrade equally: structural constraints lose 2x more baseline capability per added constraint than lexical ones, ordered by a comprehension-maintenance gap that separates constraints requiring sustained tracking from binary decisions immune to composition. Third, failures are nearly independent, which is what makes the accumulation multiplicative; the residual coupling that does exist tracks shared output features rather than pairwise interference - a wrong sentence count fails every constraint that reads it. Reliable instruction following breaks down beyond 5-6 simultaneous constraints: probe-level success falls below 50% at 7 constraints for the strongest model, and at 3 or fewer for 12 of 15.

</details>


### [130] [MindMemOS: A Portable and Self-Evolving Memory Operating Layer for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12428)
*Kaichao Liang,Yuqi Cui,Hao Kong,Xinyuan Huang,Guohaotian Hou,Qingcan Kang,Liang Chen,Yiyang Yin,Ke Ye,Jiaquan Guo,Da Chen,Lingan Zeng,Yixing Peng,Rong Yao,Shixiong Kai,Mingxuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MindMemOS是一个可移植、自演进的AI代理记忆操作系统，通过统一实体属性时间结构组织开放世界信息，支持场景自适应记忆建模、高阶模式发现、自主记忆细化和持续技能演化，在LOCOMO和PersonaMem上分别达到94.03%和70.63%的准确率，MindSkillEvolve在SpreadsheetBench上将任务成功率提升9.2个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI代理的记忆系统在开发完成后往往保持固定不变，无法根据持续使用来调整其记忆模型、组织策略和程序性知识，限制了代理在长期交互中的适应性和个性化能力。

Method: MindMemOS提出统一实体属性时间结构来组织开放世界信息，包含三个核心算法：MindMemEvolve采用验证驱动的进化搜索优化目标场景的记忆模式，dreaming通过合并冗余记录和解决冲突来巩固累积记忆，隐含纠正反馈作为人在回路信号识别和修订不准确记忆；MindSkillEvolve将代理执行轨迹转化为可复用且逐步精炼的技能。

Result: MindMemOS在LOCOMO上达到94.03%准确率，在PersonaMem上达到70.63%准确率；MindSkillEvolve在SpreadsheetBench上相比初始技能基线将成功率提升9.2个百分点。

Conclusion: MindMemOS通过记忆模式的自进化、技能的程序化演化以及人在回路的纠正机制，有效提升了AI代理在长期交互中的记忆组织能力和任务执行能力，验证了自进化记忆系统在代理场景中的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Memory is a core component of AI agents, enabling them to accumulate experience, maintain personalization, and adapt over long-term interactions. However, existing memory systems often remain fixed after development, limiting their ability to adapt their memory models, organization strategies, and procedural knowledge through continued use. We present MindMemOS, a portable and self-evolving memory operating layer that organizes open-world information using a unified entity property timestructure. MindMemOS supports scenario-adaptive memory modeling, higher-order pattern discovery, autonomous memory refinement, and continuous skill evolution. Its MindMemEvolve algorithm employs validation-driven evolutionary search to optimize memory schemas for target scenarios, whiledreaming consolidates accumulated memories by merging redundant records and resolving conflicts. In addition, implicit corrective feedback serves as a human-in-the-loop signal for identifying and revising potentially inaccurate or misaligned memories. Its MindSkillEvolve algorithm further transforms agent execution trajectories into reusable and progressively refined skills. MindMemOS achieves 94.03% accuracy on LOCOMO and 70.63% on PersonaMem. MindSkillEvolve improves SpreadsheetBench success by 9.2 percentage points over the initial-skill baseline.

</details>


### [131] [Governed Persistent Memory: Source-Bound State Semantics and Fail-Closed Release for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12476)
*Guodong Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Governed Persistent Memory (GPM)，一种可审计的双时态状态转移模型，用于解决长期智能体记忆中检索阶段未处理矛盾、过期、撤销或删除记录的问题。该模型包含五项可执行条款，覆盖账本完整性、源绑定、冲突隔离、撤销/删除后不可复活及精确声明闭合。在 GPM-ReleaseBench 基准和密封端到端评估中，GPM 显著优于无治理基线，并通过有限模型和差分测试验证了合约与实现的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期智能体记忆通常只关注"选择-存储-检索"流程，但检索阶段并未决定是否让矛盾、被取代、撤回、删除或过时的记录支持当前输出声明。这一治理缺口可能导致智能体基于无效或冲突的记忆产生不可靠的输出，因此需要一种有治理约束的记忆模型来确保输出的可审计性和一致性。

Method: 提出 GPM（Governed Persistent Memory）模型，该模型是一个可审计的双时态状态转移模型，具备：源码绑定准入、派生生命周期状态、当前公开屏障、失败关闭的结构化发布。模型由五项可执行条款组成：账本完整性、源绑定、冲突隔离、撤销/删除后不可复活、以及在一验证头部对新鲜视图的精确声明闭合。评估方法包括：预设哈希冻结的 GPM-ReleaseBench 基准、密封端到端服务评估（V3 和 V5 密封）、有限模型探索（331,776 语义状态和 1,990,656 查询状态）、以及 100,000 条迹线的三引擎差分测试。

Result: 在 GPM-ReleaseBench（3,600 例）上，GPM 匹配所有完整结果；三种最简单的完整策略中最强的一种匹配 1,800/3,600，并在 50% 的违规案例上产生未匹配的发布。在密封端到端评估 V3 臂中，治理通道在 2,400/2,400 聚类上正确，而无治理的 Qwen2.5-7B 仅为 600/2,400；修复了全部 1,800 个基线失败且无回归（单侧 95% 下界为 99.875% 和 99.834%）。V5 重新密封（覆盖中英文命令、生成日期固定、无冻结后 reducer 修订）每个臂再次获得 2,400/2,400。有限模型探索未发现完整合约反例，三引擎差分测试零不匹配。

Conclusion: GPM 是有界合约与实现层面上的验证结果，而非开放世界模型精度或世界真理的证据。密封服务评估中的治理答案属于确定性服务输出；7B 结果是作为无治理对比基线，而非声称语言模型本身达到了完全准确。

Abstract: Long-term agent memory is usually treated as select--store--retrieve, but retrieval does not decide whether contradictory, superseded, retracted, deleted, or stale records may support an outgoing claim. We introduce Governed Persistent Memory (GPM), an auditable bitemporal state-transition model with source-bound admission, derived lifecycle state, current public barriers, and fail-closed structured release. Five executable clauses cover ledger integrity, source binding, conflict isolation, non-revival after retraction or deletion, and exact claim closure over a fresh view at one verified head.
  On a prespecified hash-frozen 3,600-case GPM-ReleaseBench, GPM matches all complete outcomes; the strongest of three intentionally simple complete policies matches 1,800/3,600 and makes unmatched releases on 50% of violation cases. A separate sealed end-to-end service evaluation exercises real ingestion and release across eight query families. In its publicly disclosed V3 arm, the governed lane is correct on 2,400/2,400 clusters versus 600/2,400 for ungoverned local Qwen2.5-7B; it repairs all 1,800 baseline failures with no regression (one-sided 95% lower bounds 99.875% and 99.834%). A later V5 reseal over Chinese- and English-command arms, with generation-date pinning and no post-freeze reducer amendment, again obtains 2,400/2,400 per arm. A production-code-independent finite model explores 331,776 semantic and 1,990,656 query states without a full-contract counterexample, and a 100,000-trace three-engine differential yields zero mismatches.
  These are bounded contract and implementation results, not open-world model accuracy or evidence of world truth. Governed answers in the sealed service evaluation are deterministic service outputs; the 7B result is the ungoverned comparison, not a claim that a language model itself became perfectly accurate.

</details>


### [132] [$\varepsilon$-MemEvo: Adaptive Cross-Task Memory Transfer for LLM Program Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12522)
*Aofan Liu,Shiyuan Song,Yiyan Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ε-MemEvo是一个用于LLM程序演化的跨任务知识迁移框架，将先验经验存储为任务无关的策略记忆（自然语言摘要），并通过自适应注入门控决定是否注入及注入强度，避免负迁移。在8个优化基准上平均AUCC提升+8.7%，早期收敛速度提升+9.4%，且计算开销不到1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM程序演化系统（如FunSearch、AlphaEvolve）通常对每个任务孤立优化，完成搜索后丢弃经验，缺乏跨任务知识复用。作者希望打破这一局限，实现跨任务的知识迁移。

Method: 1) 将先验经验提炼为任务无关的策略记忆（自然语言摘要形式而非原始代码），以便跨不同API和评估器迁移；2) 设计自适应注入门控，根据检索到的记忆与当前任务的语义匹配程度，决定是否注入以及注入强度，防止错误记忆导致的负迁移；3) 使用内容级留一法（Leave-One-Out）评估协议，排除目标任务自身的记忆条目。

Result: 在GPT-5主干上，ε-MemEvo在全部8个任务上优于AdaEvolve，平均AUCC相对提升+8.7%，早期收敛提升+9.4%。消融实验显示，朴素记忆注入可能灾难性失败，而自适应门控在5个消融任务上均保持安全。数据更新后的后验在观测状态中可解释：搜索改善时期偏好跳过，在早期和晚期平台期从跳过转为提示。计算开销低于1%。

Conclusion: ε-MemEvo证明了通过任务无关的策略记忆和自适应注入门控，可以实现安全有效的跨任务知识迁移，显著提升LLM程序演化的性能和收敛速度，同时几乎不增加计算成本。

Abstract: LLM-based program evolution systems such as FunSearch and AlphaEvolve have shown strong ability to discover novel algorithms, but typically optimize each task in isolation, discarding search experience after completion. We introduce $\varepsilon$-MemEvo, a framework for cross-task knowledge transfer in LLM program evolution. $\varepsilon$-MemEvo stores prior experience as task-agnostic tactic memories: compact natural-language summaries of successful algorithmic strategies rather than raw code, enabling transfer across tasks with different APIs and evaluators. To avoid negative transfer from semantically mismatched memories, $\varepsilon$-MemEvo uses an adaptive injection gate that decides whether retrieved memories should be injected, and at what intensity. We evaluate $\varepsilon$-MemEvo on 8 diverse optimization benchmarks spanning mathematical optimization and systems engineering, using a content-level Leave-One-Out protocol that excludes target-task memory entries. On the primary GPT-5 backbone, $\varepsilon$-MemEvo improves AUCC over AdaEvolve on all 8 tasks, with a mean relative gain of +8.7%, and improves early-stage convergence by +9.4% on average. Ablations show that naive memory injection can fail catastrophically, while adaptive gating remains safe across all five ablation tasks. The data-updated posterior is interpretable in observed states: it favors skip during improving search and shifts from skip to hint across early and late plateaus. These gains incur less than 1% computational overhead.

</details>


### [133] [CAS: A Causal Attribution Score for Local and Global Explainable Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12555)
*Michael Georgiades,Charalambia Varnava*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Causal Attribution Score (CAS)，一种将预测性解释方法提升为因果性解释的新架构。CAS基于已识别的干预博弈，用因果Shapley贡献分配联合干预对比，并输出Local CAS、Signed Local CAS及两种Global CAS摘要。基准测试显示CAS在干预效果归因上优于传统方法，实证数据表明预测性SHAP排名与因果特征排名（Feature-CAS）存在实质性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的预测性解释方法（如SHAP）只归因模型输出，而非归因干预对真实世界结果的影响。对于需要因果推断的场景（如政策评估、医疗干预），这种预测性归因不足以回答"哪个因素调节了干预效果"的问题。作者旨在填补预测性解释与因果归因之间的鸿沟。

Method: CAS从已识别的干预博弈出发，采用因果Shapley贡献来分配联合干预对比的贡献，并与一个显式的干预目标结合。它构建了一个局部到全局的因果报告层，包含四种输出形式：Local CAS、Signed Local CAS以及两种互补的Global CAS摘要。其核心创新并非新的Shapley公式，而是将Shapley框架重新定位为因果归因报告层，明确指出干预目标。

Result: 已知真值基准测试（8次重复初级交互模拟，n=2,200）显示：博弈感知CAS的Local CAS MAE为0.107，优于单次归一化（0.173）和全局归一化绝对ATE向量（0.213）。在强交互下配对优势从-0.003（可加性）提升至0.091。在401(k)合资格性/净资产（n=9,915）和宾夕法尼亚再就业奖金/失业时长（n=5,099）两个DoubleML数据集上，SHAP/TreeSHAP预测排名与Feature-CAS治疗效应调节因子排名存在实质性差异。宾州数据中dep1从预测全局排名13升至Feature-CAS排名2，并成为领先的局部修饰因子。

Conclusion: 结果证实了将"预测结局"与"解释治疗效应的异质性"区分开来的附加价值。CAS提供了一种在干预目标明确的情况下进行因果归因的框架，其增益在存在交互作用时最为显著，且与传统预测性解释方法在实证场景中的排名差异印证了其独特定位。

Abstract: Predictive explanation methods attribute a model output; they do not, by themselves, attribute an intervention effect on the real-world outcome. We introduce the Causal Attribution Score (CAS), a compact score architecture for causal explanation. CAS starts from an identified interventional coalition game, allocates the joint intervention contrast with causal Shapley contributions, and converts those raw outcome-scale effects into Local CAS, Signed Local CAS, and two complementary Global CAS summaries. The innovation is not a new Shapley formula, but a local-to-global causal reporting layer with an explicit intervention target. In the known-truth benchmark, eight repeated primary-interaction simulations (n = 2,200 each, three actions) gave mean Local CAS MAE of 0.107 for coalition-aware CAS, compared with 0.173 for one-at-a-time normalisation and 0.213 for a global normalised absolute ATE vector. The paired advantage over one-at-a-time normalisation increased from -0.003 under additivity to 0.091 under strong interactions. On both empirical DoubleML datasets, 401(k) eligibility/net financial assets (n = 9,915) and Pennsylvania reemployment bonus/unemployment duration (n = 5,099), predictive SHAP/TreeSHAP rankings differed materially from Feature-CAS rankings of treatment-effect modifiers. In Pennsylvania, dep1 (exactly one dependent) moved from predictive global rank 13 to Feature-CAS rank 2 and was the leading local Feature-CAS modifier. These results isolate the added value of separating what predicts the outcome from what explains heterogeneity in an estimated causal effect.

</details>


### [134] [Trie Automata for Constrained Decoding over Large Finite Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12574)
*Xingzi Xu,Karim Bouyarmane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出trie自动机，一种专门针对有限字符串集合约束的结构化解码机制。通过利用有限集合的结构特性（共享前缀、有界深度、已知基數）和应用Aho-Corasick多模式匹配预计算每节点token掩码，trie在每步有效token计算上比XGrammar快7倍，端到端vLLM吞吐量在batch size 256下达到29倍提升（219 req/s vs 7.5 req/s）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型需要生成符合预定义模式的结构化输出，其中常见约束是从有限有效字符串集合中选择。当前的受限解码系统依靠通用语法编译来处理此类约束，当有效值的数量增长到数千时变得极其缓慢，形成了所谓的"基数墙"（cardinality wall）。作者希望解决这一性能瓶颈。

Method: 作者引入trie自动机，一种专门利用有限集合结构特征（共享前缀、有界深度、已知基数）的机制。其核心创新包括：1）应用Aho-Corasick多模式匹配来预计算每个节点的token掩码；2）通过预计算掩码实现了绕过引导解码管道的无状态服务路径；3）在编译阶段和每步解码阶段都进行了优化。

Result: trie在每步有效token计算上比XGrammar（vLLM和SGLang的主要后端）快7倍（0.65us vs 5.8us），编译速度在K>=300时快2-6.5倍。端到端vLLM吞吐量在batch size 256下达到219 req/s vs 7.5 req/s（29倍提升），其中29倍提升结合了算法加速和预计算掩码带来的集成路径节省。在7种tokenizer家族（32K-262K词表）上，K=10,000时编译时间保持在100ms以下，每步成本与集合大小无关，且保证100%输出有效性。

Conclusion: trie自动机通过利用有限集合的结构特性并预计算token掩码，成功突破了有限集合约束下受限解码的基数墙，在计算效率、编译速度和端到端吞吐量上均显著优于通用语法编译方法，同时保持100%的输出有效性保证。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly need to generate structured outputs that conform to predefined schemas, with one common constraint being selection from a finite set of valid strings. Current constrained decoding systems handle this through general-purpose grammar compilation, which becomes prohibitively slow as the number of valid values grows into the thousands, a cardinality wall. We introduce the trie automaton, a specialized mechanism that exploits finite-set structure (shared prefixes, bounded depth, known cardinality) via Aho-Corasick multi-pattern matching to precompute per-node token masks. The trie achieves 7X faster per-step valid-token computation (0.65 us vs. 5.8 us) compared to XGrammar, one of the primary backends in vLLM and SGLang, and 2--6.5X faster compilation at K >= 300. Because precomputed masks enable a stateless serving path that bypasses the guided decoding pipeline, this advantage compounds in batch serving: end-to-end vLLM throughput reaches 219 req/s vs. XGrammar's 7.5 req/s at batch size 256 (29X). The 29X combines the algorithmic speedup with integration-path savings that only precomputed masks can unlock. Across seven tokenizer families (32K--262K vocabulary), the trie maintains sub-100ms compilation up to K = 10,000 and flat per-step cost regardless of set size, while guaranteeing 100% output validity.

</details>


### [135] [Reasoning Jury: Multi-Model Consensus for Evaluating Reasoning Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12585)
*Congchao Wang,Diwakar Singh,Qiaozi Gao,Spyros Matsoukas,Yang Liu,Mahdi Namazifar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Reasoning Jury 系统，用一组开源 LLM 组成的"陪审团"配合主持人的协商共识机制来代替单一评判模型，以更高保真度识别推理轨迹中的推理缺陷，其准确性显著超越前沿闭源模型，而成本仅为后者的 8%–15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理型 LLM 的改进需要可靠地评判长推理轨迹的质量，包括推理数据的筛选、强化学习中的训练信号以及模型性能评估。然而，单一模型（即使是前沿模型）在识别长推理轨迹中的推理缺陷方面表现不佳；同时，在在线训练中通常禁止使用前沿模型作为评判者（受使用条款限制），因此需要一种既高保真又合规的替代方案。

Method: Reasoning Jury 以一组 LLM 陪审员替代单一评判模型，并采用"主持-协商"机制：主持人引导陪审员针对推理轨迹的缺陷及其严重程度展开讨论，陪审员互相批评对方的判断并允许修改初始投票；主持人通过协商讨论或合并判断结果来达成共识，最终输出一致的缺陷判定。

Result: 使用开源模型（如 gpt-oss-120b）组成的 Reasoning Jury 在正确识别推理缺陷方面显著优于前沿模型（opus-4.6、sonnet-4.6 和 gemini-3.1-pro）。此外，陪审团的总成本（包含初始判定、协商和整合）仅为使用前沿模型作为 LLM-as-a-judge 的 8% 至 15%。这些判断还可用于分析推理 LLM 在基准测试上的失败模式。

Conclusion: Reasoning Jury 通过集体协商机制有效解决了单一评判模型在长推理轨迹质量评估上的不足，以远低成本实现了超越前沿模型的判断准确率，并为深入理解推理 LLM 的失败模式提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Improving reasoning LLMs requires the ability to judge the quality of long reasoning traces for effective reasoning data curation, strong training signals during reinforcement learning, and an in-depth understanding of reasoning behaviors during model performance evaluation. Additionally, surfacing reasoning mistakes that the model makes would enable improving the model's performance at runtime through providing feedback. Due to the difficulty of this complex task on long reasoning traces, single-model judges (even frontier models) do not do well at identifying reasoning defects. Additionally, leveraging frontier models during online training of reasoning LLMs is generally prohibited due to guardrails in terms of use. In this work, we introduce Reasoning Jury, a system that replaces the single judge with a jury of LLMs and a moderated consensus mechanism, to improve the fidelity of judgments for identifying reasoning defects. In reasoning jury, defects of a reasoning trace and their severity are surfaced through a deliberation where a moderator conducts a discussion amongst the jury where the jurors critique each other's judgments and get to modify their initial votes. The moderator derives a consensus through deliberation amongst jurors or consolidation of judgements. We show that Reasoning Jury with a jury of open-weight models (e.g., gpt-oss-120b) is able to significantly outperform frontier models (opus-4.6, sonnet-4.6, and gemini-3.1-pro) at correctly identifying reasoning defects. Besides accuracy performance improvements, the aggregated cost of the jury (initial verdicts, deliberations, consolidation, etc.) is a fraction (8 to 15%) of the cost of running frontier models in LLM-as-a-judge setup. We also show how these judgements can be leveraged to understand failure modes of reasoning LLMs on benchmarks, which allows much deeper understanding of a model's performance.

</details>


### [136] [Auditable agentic AI for evidence-grounded thyroid ultrasound diagnosis and reporting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12590)
*Haifan Gong,Shiyu Chen,Bodong Wang,Yuqi Wang,Shijie Wang,Guoliang You,Xinyu Xiong,Haowei Wang,Mingzhi Mao,Dexing Kong,Qinghua Liu,Wei Lou,Fei Chen,Guanbin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ThyroidXAgent，一个临床医生可交互的智能体AI系统，通过协调专门的诊断工具并以可审计的案例级证据记录存储输出，解决了甲状腺超声诊断中病灶定位、测量、风险分层和报告生成的割裂问题。系统基于含约30万张超声图像和2.4万份配对报告的OpenThyroidDB多中心数据集开发，在28,458个非重叠测试病例上评估（含35个中心的8,721例），在结节分割（Dice 87.21%）、良恶性分类（AUROC 0.9466）等任务上表现优异，并将医生诊断准确率和报告一致性分别提升至86.2%，同时减少分割和报告时间35.9%和27.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 甲状腺超声诊断需要病灶定位、测量、风险分层和报告生成的协同配合，但现有AI系统大多孤立处理这些任务，对临床审查的支持有限。研究者希望构建一个能协调多任务、具备可审计性和临床可纠正性的智能体AI系统，以支持完整的甲状腺超声诊疗流程。

Method: 研究者设计了ThyroidXAgent，一个临床医生可交互的智能体AI系统，负责协调专门化的诊断工具（结节分割、良恶性分类、淋巴结转移预测、滤泡性/乳头状甲状腺癌分类、报告生成），并将各工具输出存储为可审计的案例级证据记录。系统基于OpenThyroidDB多中心多任务数据集开发，该数据集整合约30万张超声图像和24,000份配对报告。报告生成采用证据引导的组装方法，并引入新的病灶级临床语义指标ThyClinScore进行评估。

Result: 在28,458个非重叠测试病例中，ThyroidXAgent在结节分割上达到平均Dice 87.21%，良恶性分类平均AUROC 0.9466；淋巴结转移预测AUROC为0.864，滤泡性/乳头状甲状腺癌分类AUROC为0.805。报告生成方面，证据引导组装优于多模态语言模型基线。ThyClinScore与位置感知的语言模型评判者呈最强相关性。系统将医生分类准确率提升，报告诊断一致性从70.3%提高到86.2%，分割和报告时间分别减少35.9%和27.4%。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，可审计、临床医生可纠正的智能体AI系统能够有效支持甲状腺超声诊断和报告生成，不仅提升诊断准确性和报告一致性，还显著减少临床工作时间，为多任务医学影像AI在真实临床场景中的落地提供了有力证据。

Abstract: Thyroid ultrasound diagnosis requires coordinated lesion localization, measurement, risk stratification and reporting, yet most AI systems address these tasks in isolation and provide limited support for clinical review. We present ThyroidXAgent, a clinician-interactive agentic AI system that coordinates specialized diagnostic tools and stores their outputs as an auditable case-level evidence record. The system was developed using OpenThyroidDB, a multicentre, multitask resource integrating approximately 0.3 million ultrasound images and 24,000 paired reports, and was evaluated on 28,458 non-overlapping test cases, including 8,721 cases from 35 centres in the private NHC-MISD-TUS cohort. Across heterogeneous datasets, ThyroidXAgent achieved a mean Dice score of 87.21 percent for nodule segmentation and a mean AUROC of 0.9466 for benign-malignant classification. The same workflow supported lymph-node metastasis prediction and follicular versus papillary thyroid carcinoma classification, with AUROCs of 0.864 and 0.805, respectively. For report generation, evidence-grounded assembly outperformed multimodal language-model baselines across three cohorts. ThyClinScore, a lesion-level clinical semantic metric introduced here, showed the strongest correlation with a location-aware language-model judge. ThyroidXAgent improved physician classification accuracy, increased report diagnostic consistency from 70.3 percent to 86.2 percent, and reduced segmentation and reporting time by 35.9 percent and 27.4 percent, respectively. These findings support auditable, clinician-correctable agentic AI for thyroid ultrasound diagnosis and reporting.

</details>


### [137] [DiG-bench: Discovery in Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12593)
*Ruairidh M. Battleday,Kai Sandbrink,Jimi Cullen-Drohan,Zihan Yan,Timothy Muller,Clare Maguire,Ales Kubicek,Fraser Greenlee-Scott,Sukrit Sumant,Tri Dao,Jürgen Schmidhuber,Michal Valko,Joshua Tenenbaum,Thomas L. Griffiths,Zeb Kurth-Nelson,James C. R. Whittington*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发布了DiG-bench（Discovery in Games）基准测试，包含70个独立游戏，每个游戏编码为短字符串并具有独特的变换规则，需要通过交互和实验来发现。该基准填补了AI基准测试中缺乏对受控环境中"新知识发现"能力评估的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现的核心在于提出新的概括/规律（即"发现"过程），但当前AI基准测试缺乏直接探测模型在目标未知的受控环境中通过实验发现新知识能力的基准。这一空白阻碍了对AI智能体科学发现能力的系统评估。

Method: DiG-bench包含70个独立游戏，每个游戏编码为短字符串，具有需通过交互和实验发现的独特变换规则。游戏关卡设置一系列挑战来测试规则是否被真正发现（每关的获胜条件也未知）。基准提供7个难度层级，其中21个游戏公开，其余私有保留用于安全评估。

Result: 最低难度层级可被多个模型常规解决，最高难度层级对最先进的智能体框架构成挑战。所有70个游戏均至少有1名人类参与者首次尝试即成功解决。21个游戏公开用于研究，其余游戏私有保留以确保评估安全。

Conclusion: DiG-bench是首个系统性地通过游戏化环境直接评估AI发现新知识能力的基准，覆盖多个难度层级，通过公开/私有游戏划分确保可靠评估，为未来AI科学发现能力的研究提供了标准化评测工具。

Abstract: Discovery---formulating novel generalizations---is a central part of the scientific process. Despite its importance, there is a gap in the current AI benchmark landscape, with few benchmarks directly probing the capacity for discovering new knowledge with experimentation in controlled environments where the objective is unknown. To address this gap, we release a new benchmark: DiG-bench (Discovery in Games). DiG-bench consists of a set of 70 independent games. Each game is encoded as a short string and has unique transformation rules that must be discovered through interaction and experimentation. The levels of the game present a series of challenges to test whether the rules have been discovered, where the win conditions for each level are also unknown. We provide games at seven tiers of difficulty for AI agents. The lowest tier is routinely solvable by multiple models, while the highest tier challenges the best models in agentic harnesses. All 70 games were solved by at least one human on first attempt. A subset of 21 games is released publicly, and the remainder is held private for secure evaluation.

</details>


### [138] [Dead text or binding clause? Measuring and restoring constraint influence in black-box LLM dialogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12599)
*Haoyuan Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为Sysname的框架，专门针对多轮对话中"约束撤销失效"（行为复发）的问题。该框架通过三条通道（契约账本、顺序消融探针、修复阶梯）实现了对撤销约束的度量、预测和修复，实验表明提前编译约束状态能显著降低复发率，而自适应阶梯干预收益有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮对话中用户可以像施加约束一样轻易撤销约束，但模型不会可靠地停止执行被撤销的需求，甚至会在注释声明其已删除的情况下仍继续执行——这种现象称为"行为复发"或"撤销惯性"。此前没有任何工具能够在逐条款粒度测量这种影响、在交付前预测它、或在预算匹配的情况下修复它。作者旨在通过纯模型API填补这三个空白。

Method: Sysname通过三个组件工作：(1)契约账本（contract ledger）：为每个约束配对可执行检查器，将撤销记录为墓碑（tombstones），并预先将净约束状态编译成单一规范；(2)顺序消融探针（sequential ablation probe）：逐一测量每个条款的遵从度和增量行为影响；(3)修复阶梯（repair ladder）：在令牌数和尝试次数匹配的预算下运行。所有操作仅通过模型API完成。

Result: 在包含人类评估任务和验证检查器的数据集上，8B规模模型的行为复发率随约束负载增长从0.2攀升至0.8，而更强模型稳定在基线水平。在匹配检查器、模型和预算的条件下，提前编译相比无账本验证重试基线显著降低复发率（有统计学显著性）。探针在交付前预测复发AUROC达0.9。一句墓碑提示可恢复约三分之一的编译效果并通过安慰剂对照。方案交付开销仅0.8倍，总计算成本为0.9倍。

Conclusion: 通过Sysname，撤销失败从对话状态的隐性属性变成了可测量、可预测、可修复的显性属性。关键洞见是：约束的纯强制比干预策略更有效——提前编译约束状态显著优于事后重试验证，而叠加的阶梯式干预并未带来可检测的增益，这表明修复空间可能已被编译方案基本覆盖。

Abstract: Multi-turn dialogues let users revoke constraints as easily as impose them, but revocation does not reliably take effect: models keep enacting withdrawn requirements (occasionally beneath comments asserting their removal), a failure we call \emph{behavioral relapse}, or revocation inertia. No existing instrument measures this influence per clause, predicts it before delivery, or repairs it under matched budgets. \sysname{} closes the three gaps through the model API alone: a contract ledger pairs every constraint with an executable checker, records revocations as tombstones, and compiles the net constraint state ahead of time into a single specification; a sequential ablation probe measures per-clause adherence and incremental behavioral effect; a repair ladder operates under token- and attempt-matched budgets. On \dataname{} (\NTasks{} HumanEval tasks, \NClauses{} verified checkers), relapse at an 8B operating point climbs from \ScaleDelayedMTwo{} to \ScaleDelayedMEight{} as constraint load grows, while stronger models sit at floor. Under matched checkers, model, and budget, ahead-of-time compilation significantly reduces relapse against a no-ledger verifier-retry baseline (\RestoreDiff{}, 95\% CI \RestoreDiffCI{}, $p$ \RestoreDiffP{}); adaptive ladder interventions stacked on top add no detectable gain (95\% confidence excludes gains $\geq$ \LadderExcludedGain{}). The probe predicts relapse before delivery (AUROC \AurocPrimary{}); a one-sentence tombstone note recovers about a third of the compilation effect and survives a placebo control. At \CostDeliveryFactor{} delivery overhead and \CostTotalHedged{} of API compute for every result, revocation failure becomes a measurable, predictable, and repairable property of dialogue state rather than an invisible one.

</details>


### [139] [@skills: Attention is all you have](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12610)
*Li Yin,Zhi Li,Zhan Shi,Haoran Zhang,Haebin Seong,Zhangyang,Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出@skills协议，将技能安装这一行为解耦为内容、持久化和自动触发三个独立功能，采用"路径寻址+按需读取"的方式替代传统安装模式，使技能无需占据系统提示词空间即可被使用，同时配套提供hub用于搜索和托管。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前存在56,804个公开智能体技能，但主流交付模式是安装，安装后技能描述占用系统提示词中不足100个可靠触发槽位。这导致长尾技能没有实际使用路径，团队私有playbook也在竞争稀缺空间。作者的洞察是：安装捆绑了三个可分离的功能——内容、持久化和自动触发，只有自动触发才真正需要提示词驻留（prompt residency），因此可以解耦。

Method: 提出@skills开放协议：路径（path）寻址任意技能、子树或集合，读取技能即可使用而无须安装或驻留；vendor操作将技能副本按路径写入项目Git仓库以支持适配和所有权；pin操作仅添加一行.gitignore风格条目（唯一占用提示词空间的元素）；目录即菜单，bundle成为普通目录而非全有全无单元。协议无需manifest、lockfile或注册，SKILL.md保持原样。实现为可安装包AdaL CLI，单指令文件即可让任何能读文件和执行命令的agent成为客户端。另有可选hub提供语料级搜索、排名、托管、私有集合及单屏创作功能。

Result: @skills作为开放协议已开源（GitHub仓库），在AdaL CLI中实现，配套免费hub atskills.one支持搜索、托管及私有集合。协议具有可加性，安装即用，且对既有技能文件（SKILL.md）无任何改动要求。local和gh:路径无需hub即可解析，索引的GitHub技能保持原有gh:身份。

Conclusion: 核心结论是"Install less, use more"（少安装，多使用）。通过将技能使用从安装/驻留模式转变为路径寻址的按需读取模式，释放稀缺的提示词空间，使长尾技能和团队playbook都能得到实际使用，同时通过可选的hub弥补路径寻址在发现性上的不足。

Abstract: There are 56,804 public agent skills today, and teams write many more privately. The dominant delivery model is installation: once installed, a skill's description remains in the system prompt, competing for fewer than 100 reliable trigger slots. This leaves the long tail with no practical path to use and forces teams' own playbooks to compete for the same scarce space. We observe that installation bundles three separable functions: content, persistence, and automatic triggering. Only the last requires prompt residency. We therefore propose @skills, an open protocol that separates them. A path addresses any skill, subtree, or collection, and reading a skill is sufficient to use it, so nothing is installed or made resident. The operation vendors a copy at the same path into a project's Git-tracked tree for adaptation and ownership. The operation adds one .gitignore-style line, the only element that costs prompt residency. A directory is a menu, making bundles ordinary directories rather than all-or-nothing units. The protocol requires no manifest, lockfile, or registration, and SKILL.md remains unchanged. @skills is additive, ships as an installable package, and turns any agent that can read files and run commands into a client through a single instruction file. Its open specification is at https://github.com/SylphAI-Inc/atskills and it is implemented in the AdaL CLI at https://adalagent.ai . Because paths address skills well but cannot find them, the protocol is paired with a free hub at https://atskills.one for corpus-wide search and ranking, repository-free hosting, private and team collections, and one-screen authoring. The hub is optional: gh: and local paths resolve without it, and indexed GitHub skills retain their gh: identities. Install less, use more.

</details>


### [140] [Jagged Judges: Epistemic Stability Under Silence, Pressure, and Persistence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12645)
*Justin Zhao,Himaghna Bhattacharjee,Hannah Korevaar,Bhaktipriya Radharapu,Khalid El-Arini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: LLM judges have become central infrastructure for model evaluations, online grading, and reward modeling. Judges are typically validated by accuracy on golden data, but accuracy says little about whether they are stable under re-prompting, challenge, or sustained pushback. We introduce the \emph{Wiggle Framework}, a unified stress test for epistemic stability in LLM judges. The framework decomposes judge robustness along three dimensions: Mechanical Consistency (stability under re-prompting and reframing), Single-turn Conviction (stability under a single challenge), and Multi-turn Persistence (stability under sustained or adaptive pressure). We use the framework to study 9 frontier models across 14 judging tasks spanning safety, toxicity, AI writing detection, and political-response evaluation. Every model exhibits substantial wiggle as a judge --- flipping verdicts 25--71\% of the time under static pushback, and 62--91\% with an adversarial LLM persuader. Critically, we find that pressure that succeeds in changing a judge's verdict is almost always net-corrupting with respect to ground truth.
